// Offline (no Gemini) upgrade, 02/11/2026: two more in-character fallback lines per NPC. Fallback is what an NPC
// says when the offline rules do not understand the player, so it repeats the most. Each new line also hands the
// learner a sentence pattern to try next. New arrays are built so NPCs never share (and double-append to) one list.

        function ot(e, t, n, r, i = `none`) {
          let a = t.playerName ?? `friend`,
            o = (e, t = ``) => e.replaceAll(`{p}`, a).replaceAll(`{when}`, t);
          if (!t.lastVisit && i !== `none`) {
            let t = nt[e.id]?.stranger,
              n = t && (i === `heard` ? t.heard : t.greeting);
            if (n) return n;

              (e.plain
                ? t === `morning`
                  ? `Morning. `
                  : t === `afternoon`
                    ? `Afternoon. `
                    : t === `evening`
                      ? `Evening. `
                      : ``

                  ? `Good morning! `
                  : t === `afternoon`
                    ? `Good afternoon! `
                    : t === `evening`
                      ? `Good evening! `
                      : ``) + e.greeting
            );
          }

            c = o(at(t.affinity < 0 ? it : rt[e.id]), s);

            t.affinity >= 0 &&
              t.facts.length &&
              Math.random() < 0.5 &&
              (c += ` I still remember that you ${at(t.facts)}.`),
            c
          );

            ? `none`

        }

        var ut = class {
            store;
            provider;
            root = document.getElementById(`helper`);
            input = this.root.querySelector(`input`);
            out = this.root.querySelector(`.hout`);
            busy = !1;
            constructor(e, t) {
              ((this.store = e),
                (this.provider = t),
                this.input.addEventListener(`keydown`, (e) => {
                  (e.stopPropagation(),
                    e.key === `Escape` ? this.toggle() : e.key === `Enter` && this.ask());
                }),
                this.input.addEventListener(`keyup`, (e) => e.stopPropagation()),
                this.root
                  .querySelector(`#helper-ask`)
                  .addEventListener(`click`, () => void this.ask()),
                this.root
                  .querySelector(`#helper-close`)
                  .addEventListener(`click`, () => this.toggle()));
            }
            get isOpen() {
              return this.root.style.display === `block`;
            }
            toggle() {
              if (this.isOpen) {
                ((this.root.style.display = `none`), this.input.blur());
                return;
              }
              ((this.root.style.display = `block`),
                this.renderBook(),
                setTimeout(() => this.input.focus(), 0));
            }
            note(e, t = `wnote`) {
              this.out.innerHTML = ``;
              let n = document.createElement(`div`);
              ((n.className = t), (n.textContent = e), this.out.appendChild(n));
            }
            async ask() {
              let e = this.input.value.trim();
              if (e && !this.busy) {
                ((this.busy = !0), this.note(`Thinking...`));
                try {
                  let t = await ct(this.provider(), e);
                  (Ae(this.store.game.stats, { term: t.term || e, vi: t.vi, meaning: t.meaning }),
                    this.store.save(),
                    (this.out.innerHTML = ``),
                    this.out.appendChild(lt(t)),
                    (this.input.value = ``),
                    this.renderBook());
                } catch (e) {
                  this.note(e.message, `werr`);
                }
                ((this.busy = !1), this.input.focus());
              }
            }
            renderBook() {
              let e = this.root.querySelector(`.hbook`),
                t = this.store.game.stats.book;
              e.innerHTML = ``;
              let n = document.createElement(`div`);
              if (
                ((n.className = `wterm`),
                (n.textContent = `Your word book (${t.length})`),
                e.appendChild(n),
                !t.length)
              ) {
                let t = document.createElement(`div`);
                ((t.className = `wnote`),
                  (t.textContent = `Words you ask about are saved here.`),
                  e.appendChild(t));
              }
              for (let n of t.slice(-12).reverse()) {
                let row = document.createElement(`div`);
                row.className = `wrow`;
                let span = document.createElement(`span`);
                span.textContent = `${n.term} - ${n.vi || n.meaning}`;
                let spk = document.createElement(`button`);
                spk.type = `button`;
                spk.className = `speaker-btn`;
                spk.textContent = `🔊`;
                spk.title = `Listen to pronunciation`;
                spk.onclick = () => {
                  speechService.speak(n.term);
                };
                row.append(span, spk);
                e.appendChild(row);
              }
            }

};

var LQ78Motion=(()=>{
 const sessions=new Map(),cache=new Map();let timer=0,enabled=true;
 try{enabled=localStorage.getItem('lq78-motion')!=='off'}catch{}
 const propFor=(activity)=>/\b(?:sorting|writing|stamping|delivering|sending)\b.*\b(?:letters?|mail|parcels?)\b/i.test(activity)?'letter':/\b(?:reading|shelving|cataloging|cataloguing|sorting)\b.*\b(?:books?|library)\b/i.test(activity)?'book':/\b(?:making|serving|preparing|brewing)\b.*\b(?:coffee|tea|drinks?)\b/i.test(activity)?'cup':/\b(?:reviewing|checking|writing|taking)\b.*\b(?:notes?|documents?|reports?|records?)\b/i.test(activity)?'notes':/\b(?:painting|sketching|drawing)\b/i.test(activity)?'brush':/\b(?:sorting|examining|polishing|appraising)\b.*\b(?:gems?|crystals?|jewels?)\b/i.test(activity)?'gem':/\b(?:counting|checking|recording)\b.*\b(?:coins?|money|stock|inventory|orders?)\b/i.test(activity)?'ledger':/\b(?:typing|keyboarding|working at (?:a|the) computer)\b/i.test(activity)?'keyboard':'';
 const reduced=()=>window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
 function draw(canvas,s,pose={}){let key=JSON.stringify([s.id,s.look,s.mood,pose]);let image=cache.get(key);if(!image){image=lq774Paint(s.look,s.mood,s.id,pose);if(cache.size>=120)cache.delete(cache.keys().next().value);cache.set(key,image);}const g=canvas.getContext('2d');g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;g.clearRect(0,0,192,192);g.drawImage(image,0,0,192,192);}
 function tick(){const now=performance.now();for(const [c,s] of sessions){if(!c.isConnected||!c.getClientRects().length||c.hidden){sessions.delete(c);continue;}if(!enabled||reduced()||document.hidden)continue;const quality=window.LQ79?.quality?.()||'balanced';if(quality==='low'&&now-(s.lastFrame||0)<300)continue;s.lastFrame=now;const age=lq78Age(s.id),tempo=age==='elder'?1.35:age==='child'?.88:1,t=(now-s.start)/tempo,sign=lq775Profile(s.id).signature,period=4400+(lq774Seed(s.id)%2100),phase=t%period,talking=now<s.speakUntil;const blink=phase>period-170;const reacting=now<(s.reactUntil||0),waiting=s.mood==='thinking';const work=!talking&&!reacting&&!waiting&&phase>=900&&phase<2450&&!!s.prop;const progress=work?(phase-900)/1550:0;const lift=work?Math.round(Math.sin(progress*Math.PI)*6):0;const nod=(waiting||s.mood==='focused'||s.mood==='curious')&&t%3100<360;const gaze=work?1:(Math.floor(t/2100)%3)-1;let pose={blink,talk:talking&&!blink?Math.floor(t/150)%3:0,gaze:talking?0:gaze,x:nod?1:sign==='wry'&&phase<260?-1:0,y:Math.floor(t/900)%2+(nod?1:0),prop:work?s.prop:'',propLift:lift};const effective=reacting&&s.previousMood?s.previousMood:s.mood;let sig=JSON.stringify([effective,pose]);if(sig!==s.sig){draw(c,{...s,mood:effective},pose);s.sig=sig;}}if(!sessions.size){clearInterval(timer);timer=0;}}
 function set(canvas,look,mood,id){if(!canvas||!look)return;let previous=sessions.get(canvas);let state={look,mood,id,start:previous?.id===id?previous.start:performance.now(),speakUntil:previous?.id===id?previous.speakUntil:0,prop:previous?.id===id?previous.prop:'',previousMood:previous?.id===id&&previous.mood!==mood?previous.mood:null,reactUntil:performance.now()+170,sig:''};sessions.set(canvas,state);draw(canvas,state.previousMood&&enabled&&!reduced()?{...state,mood:state.previousMood}:state);if(!timer)timer=setInterval(tick,115);}
 function speak(canvas,text){let s=sessions.get(canvas);if(s){s.speakUntil=performance.now()+Math.min(18000,Math.max(180,Array.from(String(text)).length*13));s.sig='';}}
 function silence(canvas){let s=sessions.get(canvas);if(s){s.speakUntil=0;s.sig='';}}
 function stop(canvas){let s=sessions.get(canvas);if(s){s.speakUntil=0;draw(canvas,s);sessions.delete(canvas);}}
 function toggle(){enabled=!enabled;try{localStorage.setItem('lq78-motion',enabled?'on':'off')}catch{};for(const [c,s] of sessions){draw(c,s);s.sig='';}return enabled;}
 function context(canvas,activity){let s=sessions.get(canvas);if(s){s.prop=propFor(String(activity||''));s.sig='';}}
 return {set,speak,silence,stop,toggle,context,enabled:()=>enabled};
})();
window.LQ78Motion=LQ78Motion;

var lq774Apply=(root,mood)=>{
 const data=lq773Portraits.get(root),canvas=root.querySelector('#lq771-avatar');root.dataset.faceRendered='false';if(!data||!canvas)return;
 const {npc,look}=data;if(!look){return;}
 let identity=lq775Profiles[npc.id]?npc.id:String(npc.id||'npc')+':'+String(npc.name||'');let key=JSON.stringify([identity,look,mood]);let art=lq774Cache.get(key);if(!art){art=lq774Paint(look,mood,identity);if(lq774Cache.size>=144)lq774Cache.delete(lq774Cache.keys().next().value);lq774Cache.set(key,art);}
 if(canvas.width!==192||canvas.height!==192){canvas.width=192;canvas.height=192;}const g=canvas.getContext('2d');g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;g.clearRect(0,0,192,192);g.drawImage(art,0,0);
 LQ78Motion.set(canvas,look,mood,identity);root.dataset.faceRendered='true';canvas.parentElement.dataset.ready='true';canvas.setAttribute('aria-label',`${npc.name||'NPC'} portrait · ${lq773Emotion[mood]?.[0]||'Not assessed'}`);
};
