var lq773ShopFacts=(npc)=>{const eco=window.LQ10_ECONOMY;if(!eco?.catalog||!eco?.shops)return [];return Object.entries(eco.catalog).filter(([id,d])=>d.shops.some(s=>eco.shops[s]?.npc===npc.id)).map(([id,d])=>({id,name:d.name.replace(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/u,''),price:d.price,shops:d.shops.filter(s=>eco.shops[s]?.npc===npc.id)}));};
var lq773FactText=(npc)=>{const items=lq773ShopFacts(npc);return items.length?items.map(d=>`${d.name}: $${d.price} (${d.shops.join(', ')})`).join('; '):'No verified shop prices for this NPC.';};
var lq773PriceReply=(npc,message,reply)=>{
 const items=lq773ShopFacts(npc);if(!items.length)return null;
 const q=String(message).toLowerCase(),r=String(reply||'');const isPrice=/\b(price|prices|cost|costs|how much|how many dollars|expensive|cheap)\b/i.test(q)||/\$\s*\d|\b(?:dollars?|bucks)\b/i.test(r);if(!isPrice)return null;
 const aliases={coffee:'coffee',tea:'tea',cafe_combo:'breakfast|combo',pizza:'pizza',bread:'bread',apple:'apple',salad:'salad',soup:'soup',dinner:'dinner',stamp:'stamp|stamps|postage',shipping:'parcel|package|shipping',cake:'cake|pastry',hotel_room:'single|standard|inn room|room|bed',hotel_suite:'suite|deluxe',rail_ticket:'train ticket|rail ticket|ticket',battery:'battery|batteries|pin',pickaxe:'pickaxe|pickax'};
 const matches=(text)=>items.filter(d=>{let words=(aliases[d.id]||d.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9 ]/g,'').trim()).split('|');return words.some(a=>a&&new RegExp('\\b'+a.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+'\\b','i').test(text));});
 let found=matches(q);if(found.length>1&&/\b(suite|deluxe)\b/.test(q))found=found.filter(x=>x.id!=='hotel_room');
 // Do not invent an amount for absent items. Generic price questions get a short authoritative menu.
 if(!found.length&&/\b(price|prices|menu|how much|cost)\b/i.test(q)&&!(/\b(for|of|does|is)\b/.test(q)))found=items.slice(0,3);
 if(!found.length)return 'I can only confirm the prices in my shop menu. You can check the shop for the current price.';
 return found.slice(0,3).map(d=>`${d.name} costs $${d.price}.`).join(' ');
};

// V79 — real encounter context, local journal and opt-in practice. No scene generation.
var LQ79=(()=>{
 const sessions=new Map(), configs=new WeakMap(); let timer=0, audio=null, quality='balanced',sound=false;
 try{quality=localStorage.getItem('lq79-quality')||quality}catch{};if(!['low','balanced','high'].includes(quality))quality='balanced';
 const q=(root,s)=>root.querySelector(s), scene=()=>window.__lifequestGetScene?.(), text=x=>String(x??'').trim(), reduced=()=>window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
 const label=(sc)=>text(sc?.inside?.b?.name)||text(sc?.area?.name)||'Location unavailable';
 const areaKey=sc=>[sc?.area?.id||sc?.area?.name||'',sc?.inside?.b?.name||'',sc?.inside?.b?.id||'',sc?.inside?.floor??''].join('|');
 const nearby=(sc,id)=>!sc?.player?[]:(sc.npcs||[]).filter(n=>n.def?.id&&n.def.id!==id&&n.sprite?.visible&&!!n.inside===!!sc.inside&&Math.hypot(n.sprite.x-sc.player.x,n.sprite.y-sc.player.y)<(sc.inside?95:80)).sort((a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.sprite.x-sc.player.x,a.sprite.y-sc.player.y)-Math.hypot(b.sprite.x-sc.player.x,b.sprite.y-sc.player.y)).slice(0,3);
 function neutral(canvas){const g=canvas.getContext('2d');g.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);canvas.width=320;canvas.height=224;const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,320,224);gr.addColorStop(0,'#394e51');gr.addColorStop(.55,'#263b40');gr.addColorStop(1,'#16292e');g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,320,224);}
 function status(s,v){if(s.badge)s.badge.textContent=v;}
 // Use only the current Phaser canvas. No invented architecture, weather or scenery.
 function view(sc,canvas,id){const cam=sc?.cameras?.main;if(!cam||!canvas?.width||!canvas.height)return null;const world=cam.worldView;const target=(sc.npcs||[]).find(n=>n.def?.id===id&&n.sprite?.visible&&!!n.inside===!!sc.inside)?.sprite||sc.player;const scaleX=canvas.width/(sc.scale?.width||canvas.width),scaleY=canvas.height/(sc.scale?.height||canvas.height);const vx=(cam.x||0)*scaleX,vy=(cam.y||0)*scaleY,vw=(cam.width||canvas.width)*scaleX,vh=(cam.height||canvas.height)*scaleY;const z=cam.zoom||1;let cx=vx+vw/2,cy=vy+vh/2;if(target&&world&&Number.isFinite(target.x)&&Number.isFinite(target.y)){cx=vx+(target.x-world.x)*z*scaleX;cy=vy+(target.y-world.y)*z*scaleY;if(cx<vx||cx>vx+vw||cy<vy||cy>vy+vh){cx=vx+vw/2;cy=vy+vh/2;}}
 const room=sc.inside?.spec,unit=32*(sc.inside?.k||1),roomW=room?.w*unit*z*scaleX,roomH=room?.h*unit*z*scaleY;const limit=Number.isFinite(roomW)&&Number.isFinite(roomH)?Math.min(roomW,roomH*10/7):Infinity;const w=Math.max(1,Math.min(limit,vw,vh*10/7,Math.max(240*scaleX,vw*.59))),h=w*7/10;return {x:Math.max(vx,Math.min(vx+vw-w,cx-w/2)),y:Math.max(vy,Math.min(vy+vh-h,cy-h*.55)),w,h};}
 function capture(s){const sc=scene();if(!sc||sc!==s.scene||areaKey(sc)!==s.key){neutral(s.canvas);status(s,'Scene unavailable');return false;}const source=sc.game?.canvas||sc.sys?.game?.canvas||document.querySelector('#game canvas');const rect=view(sc,source,s.id);if(!rect||source===s.canvas)return false;
 try{const c=s.canvas,W=quality==='high'?480:320,H=Math.round(W*.7);if(c.width!==W||c.height!==H){c.width=W;c.height=H;}const g=c.getContext('2d');g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;const temp=s.temp||(s.temp=document.createElement('canvas'));temp.width=32;temp.height=22;const t=temp.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});t.drawImage(source,rect.x,rect.y,rect.w,rect.h,0,0,32,22);const data=t.getImageData(0,0,32,22).data;let opaque=0,diverse=false,first=-1;for(let i=0;i<data.length;i+=4){if(data[i+3]>100){opaque++;const color=(data[i]<<16)+(data[i+1]<<8)+data[i+2];if(first===-1)first=color;else if(color!==first)diverse=true;}}if(opaque<100||!diverse)return false;
 g.clearRect(0,0,W,H);g.drawImage(source,rect.x,rect.y,rect.w,rect.h,0,0,W,H);const weather=document.getElementById('v8-weather-canvas');if(!sc.inside&&weather?.width&&weather.height&&getComputedStyle(weather).display!=='none'&&getComputedStyle(weather).visibility!=='hidden'){const sx=weather.width/source.width,sy=weather.height/source.height;g.drawImage(weather,rect.x*sx,rect.y*sy,rect.w*sx,rect.h*sy,0,0,W,H);}s.captured=true;const activity=q(s.root,'#lq772-activity'),clock=q(s.root,'#lq772-day');if(activity){const f=facts({id:s.id},sc);activity.textContent=f.activity||'Activity unavailable';LQ78Motion.context(q(s.root,'#lq771-avatar'),f.activity);}const day=sc.store?.game?.day,min=sc.store?.game?.minutes;if(clock&&Number.isFinite(day)&&Number.isFinite(min))clock.textContent=`DAY ${day} / ${String(Math.floor(min/60)%24).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(Math.floor(min)%60).padStart(2,'0')}`;status(s,quality==='low'||reduced()?'World snapshot':'World view');return true;}catch{return false;}}
 function request(s){if(s.pending)return;const sc=scene();if(!sc||sc!==s.scene||areaKey(sc)!==s.key){capture(s);return;}const bus=sc.game?.events||sc.sys?.game?.events;const run=()=>{s.pending=false;clearTimeout(s.wait);if(sessions.get(s.canvas)!==s)return;const ok=capture(s);if(!ok&&!s.captured){neutral(s.canvas);status(s,'Scene unavailable');}};if(bus?.once){s.pending=true;bus.once('postrender',run);s.wait=setTimeout(()=>{bus.off?.('postrender',run);run();},300);}else run();}
 function refresh(){if(audio&&(document.hidden||audio.key!==areaKey(scene())))stopSound();for(const [c,s] of sessions){if(!c.isConnected||!s.root.getClientRects().length||s.root.closest('[hidden]')){clearTimeout(s.wait);sessions.delete(c);continue;}if(s.scene!==scene()||s.key!==areaKey(scene())){neutral(c);status(s,'Scene changed');sessions.delete(c);continue;}if(!document.hidden&&(!s.captured||quality!=='low'&&!reduced())){if(performance.now()-(s.last||0)>(quality==='high'?480:1250)){s.last=performance.now();request(s);}}}if(!sessions.size){clearInterval(timer);timer=0;}}
 function attach(root,canvas,id,badge){if(!canvas)return;neutral(canvas);let s={root,canvas,id,badge,scene:scene(),key:areaKey(scene()),captured:false};sessions.set(canvas,s);status(s,'Locating scene…');if(!capture(s))request(s);if(!timer)timer=setInterval(refresh,250);}
 function detach(root){for(const [c,s] of sessions)if(s.root===root){clearTimeout(s.wait);sessions.delete(c);}if(!sessions.size){clearInterval(timer);timer=0;}}
 function facts(npc,sc=scene()){const live=(sc?.npcs||[]).find(n=>n.def?.id===npc?.id&&n.sprite?.visible&&!!n.inside===!!sc.inside);const room=label(sc),w=!sc?.inside?text(document.getElementById('v8-weather-condition')?.textContent):'';return {room,activity:text(live?.slot?.label),weather:w,nearby:nearby(sc,npc?.id).map(n=>n.def.name)};}
 function setQuality(v){if(!['low','balanced','high'].includes(v))return;quality=v;try{localStorage.setItem('lq79-quality',v)}catch{};for(const s of sessions.values()){s.captured=false;request(s);}return v;}
 // Local, optional synthesized ambience: no external file or microphone access.
 function stopSound(){if(audio){try{audio.source.stop();audio.ctx.close()}catch{}audio=null;}sound=false;}
 async function toggleSound(root){if(sound){stopSound();return false;}const sc=scene(),f=facts(null,sc);if(!sc)return false;const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return false;const rain=!sc.inside&&/rain|mưa/i.test(f.weather);const room=!!sc.inside;const loc=f.room.toLowerCase(),water=/waterfall|river|thác|sông/.test(loc),market=/market|bazaar|shop|chợ/.test(loc),office=/office|library|study|thư viện/.test(loc);try{const ctx=new AC();const arr=ctx.createBuffer(1,ctx.sampleRate*2,ctx.sampleRate),d=arr.getChannelData(0);let last=0;for(let i=0;i<d.length;i++){last=(last+.019*(Math.random()*2-1))/1.019;d[i]=last*3;}const node=ctx.createBufferSource();node.buffer=arr;node.loop=true;const filter=ctx.createBiquadFilter();filter.type='lowpass';filter.frequency.value=rain?1700:water?1100:market?850:office?320:room?240:700;const gain=ctx.createGain();gain.gain.value=(rain||water)?.075:.035;if(ctx.createStereoPanner){const pan=ctx.createStereoPanner(),n=nearby(sc,null)[0]?.sprite;pan.pan.value=n&&sc.player?Math.max(-.5,Math.min(.5,(n.x-sc.player.x)/160)):0;node.connect(filter).connect(gain).connect(pan).connect(ctx.destination);}else node.connect(filter).connect(gain).connect(ctx.destination);node.start();await ctx.resume();if(!root.getClientRects().length){node.stop();ctx.close();return false;}audio={ctx,source:node,key:areaKey(sc)};sound=true;return true;}catch{stopSound();return false;}}
 function state(memory){const m=memory.lq79&&typeof memory.lq79==='object'?memory.lq79:(memory.lq79={});if(!Array.isArray(m.promises))m.promises=[];if(!Array.isArray(m.topics))m.topics=[];if(!Array.isArray(m.practice))m.practice=[];return m;}
 function record(memory,npc,input,result,situation,teacher){const a=LQ78Meaning.classify(npc,[],input);const m=state(memory),day=situation?.day;if(teacher&&result.correction&&!a.injection&&!/\b(?:password|phone number|home address|email address|api key)\b/i.test(input)){const correction=String(result.correction).slice(0,180),sample=String(input).slice(0,160);if(!m.practice.some(x=>x.correction===correction)){m.practice.push({sample,correction,day});m.practice=m.practice.slice(-8);}}if(a.quote||a.hyp||a.injection||a.sarcasm||a.neg||a.hostile||a.outside)return;const p=String(input).match(/^\s*i (?:promise (?:to|that i will)|will|plan to)\s+(.{3,100})[.!]?$/i);if(p&&!/\?|\b(?:if|unless|maybe|probably|password|address|phone|email|secret)\b/i.test(p[1])){const v=p[1].trim().replace(/[.!]$/,'');if(!m.promises.some(x=>x.text===v))m.promises.push({text:v,day});m.promises=m.promises.slice(-8);}const topic=a.price?'shop prices':a.keyword?'mission topic':a.doubt?'clarification':result.topicStatus==='smalltalk'?'small talk':result.topicStatus==='relevant'?'in-role discussion':'';if(topic){m.topics=[...m.topics.filter(t=>t!==topic),topic].slice(-6);}}
 function contextText(npc,memory){const f=facts(npc),m=state(memory);return `Verified encounter: location ${f.room}; scheduled activity ${f.activity||'unknown'}; observed weather label ${f.weather||'not available'}; nearby residents ${f.nearby.join(', ')||'none confirmed'}. Player-stated intentions (not confirmed actions): ${m.promises.map(p=>p.text).join('; ')||'none'}. Recent conversation categories: ${m.topics.join(', ')||'none'}. Never claim that an intention has been fulfilled.`;}
 function contextualReply(npc,input,memory){const a=LQ78Meaning.classify(npc,[],input);if(a.injection||a.quote||a.hyp||a.sarcasm||a.hostile)return null;const s=a.q, f=facts(npc);let reply='';if(/^(?:where are we|where am i|what (?:room|place) (?:is this|are we in))\b/.test(s))reply=f.room==='Location unavailable'?"I can't identify our current location.":`We're at ${f.room}.`;else if(/^(?:what are you doing|are you busy|what are you working on)\b/.test(s))reply=f.activity?`My current activity is ${f.activity.replace(/[.!]$/,'')}. What can I help you with?`:"I don't have a confirmed activity right now. What would you like to discuss?";else if(/^(?:who (?:is|else is) (?:nearby|here|around us)|who(?:'s| is) nearby)\b/.test(s))reply=f.nearby.length?`Nearby I can identify ${f.nearby.join(', ')}.`:"I can't confirm another resident nearby.";else if(/^(?:do you remember (?:my promise|what i promised|what i said i would do)|what did i promise)\b/.test(s)){const p=state(memory).promises.at(-1);reply=p?`You said you intended to ${p.text}. I don't know whether you've done that yet.`:"I haven't recorded a promise from you.";}else return null;return {reply,replySource:'Verified context / memory',mood:'calm',moodSource:'Local response tone',topicStatus:'relevant',goalDone:false,affinityDelta:0,remember:''};}
 const node=(tag,val,cls)=>{const n=document.createElement(tag);if(val)n.textContent=val;if(cls)n.className=cls;return n;};
 function addButton(parent,label,fn){const b=node('button',label);b.type='button';b.addEventListener('click',fn);parent.append(b);return b;}
 function refreshTools(chat){const root=chat.root,drawer=q(root,'#lq79-tools'),cfg=configs.get(root);if(!drawer||!cfg||!chat.npc)return;const panel=q(drawer,'#lq79-content');panel.replaceChildren();const f=facts(chat.npc),m=state(chat.store.get(chat.npc.id));const line=(k,v)=>{const d=node('div',null,'lq79-record');d.append(node('strong',k),node('span',v));panel.append(d);};const fill=t=>{chat.input.value=t;drawer.hidden=true;q(root,'#lq79-toggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');chat.input.focus();};
 if(cfg.tab==='scene'){line('Location',f.room);line('Current activity',f.activity||'Not confirmed');line('Weather',f.weather||'Not available');line('Nearby residents',f.nearby.join(', ')||'None confirmed');const opts=node('div',null,'lq79-actions');for(const v of ['low','balanced','high']){const b=addButton(opts,v[0].toUpperCase()+v.slice(1),()=>{setQuality(v);refreshTools(chat);});b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(quality===v));}addButton(opts,sound?'Ambience ON':'Ambience OFF',async()=>{await toggleSound(root);if(chat.npc)refreshTools(chat);});panel.append(opts);}
 if(cfg.tab==='memory'){if(!m.promises.length&&!m.topics.length)line('No entries yet','Player-stated intentions and conversation categories appear here.');for(const p of [...m.promises].reverse())line('Intention · day '+(p.day??'—'),p.text);if(m.topics.length)line('Topics',m.topics.join(' · '));addButton(panel,'Clear context notes',()=>{const n=chat.store.get(chat.npc.id);n.lq79={promises:[],topics:[],practice:n.lq79?.practice||[]};chat.store.save();refreshTools(chat);});}
 if(cfg.tab==='coach'){if(!m.practice?.length)line('No recorded corrections','Turn Teacher on and practise English.');for(const p of [...(m.practice||[])].reverse()){line(p.sample,p.correction);addButton(panel,'Try again',()=>fill(p.sample));}if(m.practice?.length)addButton(panel,'Clear practice notes',()=>{state(chat.store.get(chat.npc.id)).practice=[];chat.store.save();refreshTools(chat);});}
 if(cfg.tab==='paths'){const a=['What are you doing right now?','Could you tell me more about this place?'];if(chat.npc.goal?.text)a.unshift('Could you explain what I need to do?');if(lq773ShopFacts(chat.npc).length)a.push('What prices can you confirm?');if(m.promises.length)a.push('Do you remember my promise?');line('Choose a direction','These only fill the input. You decide what to send.');for(const v of a)addButton(panel,v,()=>fill(v));}
 if(cfg.tab==='group'){line('Nearby discussion','Choose a resident who is actually nearby. Their answer is a separate practice turn, without quest rewards.');const list=nearby(scene(),chat.npc.id);if(!list.length){line('No nearby participant','Talk to another resident when they are close.');return;}const select=node('select');select.setAttribute('aria-label','Nearby resident');for(const n of list){const o=node('option',n.def.name);o.value=n.def.id;select.append(o);}const input=node('input');input.placeholder='Ask the nearby resident…';input.maxLength=400;input.setAttribute('aria-label','Group practice question');const out=node('div',null,'lq79-record');const ask=addButton(panel,'Ask resident',async()=>{const other=nearby(scene(),chat.npc?.id).find(n=>n.def.id===select.value);const msg=input.value.trim();if(!other||!msg||chat.busy||cfg.groupBusy)return;cfg.groupBusy=true;const id=chat.npc.id,session=chat.lq773Session;ask.disabled=true;out.textContent='Waiting for '+other.def.name+'…';try{const situation=chat.situation(other.def),memory=chat.store.get(other.def.id);const h=[...chat.history.slice(-4),...(cfg.discussion||[]).flatMap(t=>[{role:'user',text:t.question||''},{role:'npc',text:`${t.name}: ${t.reply}`}]),{role:'npc',text:'We are discussing this with '+chat.npc.name+'.'}];const local=contextualReply(other.def,msg,memory);const response=local||await chat.provider.respond(other.def,h,msg,true,{...situation,memory,prior:memory.transcript||[],gossip:[],stranger:'none',lq79Context:contextText(other.def,memory)});if(chat.lq773Session!==session||chat.npc?.id!==id||!out.isConnected)return;if(!nearby(scene(),id).some(n=>n.def.id===other.def.id)){out.textContent='That resident has moved away.';return;}const reply=LQ78Meaning.guard(other.def,h,msg,{...jt(response,true,other.def,h),replySource:response.replySource||chat.provider.label},true);out.textContent=other.def.name+' · '+(reply.replySource||'Practice')+'\n'+reply.reply;cfg.discussion=[...(cfg.discussion||[]),{name:other.def.name,question:msg,reply:reply.reply}].slice(-3);LQ80.noteGroup(chat,other.def);input.value='';}catch{if(out.isConnected)out.textContent='Could not get a reply. Try again.';}finally{cfg.groupBusy=false;ask.disabled=false;}});panel.insertBefore(select,ask);panel.insertBefore(input,ask);panel.append(out);for(const turn of cfg.discussion||[])line(turn.name,turn.reply);}
 }
 function setup(chat){const root=chat.root,drawer=q(root,'#lq79-tools');if(!drawer)return;configs.set(root,{tab:'scene',discussion:[]});const toolbar=q(root,'#lq79-toggle');toolbar.addEventListener('click',()=>{drawer.hidden=!drawer.hidden;toolbar.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!drawer.hidden));if(!drawer.hidden)refreshTools(chat);});for(const b of drawer.querySelectorAll('[data-lq79-tab]'))b.addEventListener('click',()=>{const cfg=configs.get(root);cfg.tab=b.dataset.lq79Tab;for(const r of drawer.querySelectorAll('[data-lq79-tab]'))r.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(r===b));refreshTools(chat);});q(drawer,'#lq79-close').addEventListener('click',()=>{drawer.hidden=true;toolbar.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');chat.input.focus();});drawer.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{e.stopPropagation();if(e.key==='Escape'){drawer.hidden=true;toolbar.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');chat.input.focus();}});}
 function open(chat){const root=chat.root,drawer=q(root,'#lq79-tools');if(drawer){drawer.hidden=true;q(root,'#lq79-toggle')?.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');const c=configs.get(root);if(c){c.discussion=[];c.groupBusy=false;}} }
 function close(root){detach(root);stopSound();const drawer=q(root,'#lq79-tools');if(drawer)drawer.hidden=true;}
 function regional(panel,npc,activity){const section=q(panel,'.lq775-regional');if(!section)return;let bg=q(section,'.lq79-regional-bg');if(!bg){bg=node('canvas',null,'lq79-regional-bg');bg.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');section.prepend(bg);}let badge=q(section,'.lq79-regional-label');if(!badge){badge=node('small',null,'lq79-regional-label');section.append(badge);}attach(panel,bg,npc.id,badge);const avatar=q(section,'canvas:not(.lq79-regional-bg)');if(avatar)LQ78Motion.context(avatar,activity||'');}
 return {attach,detach,view,facts,nearby,setQuality,quality:()=>quality,record,contextText,contextualReply,setup,open,close,regional,refreshTools};
})();
window.LQ79=LQ79;
/* LIFEQUEST AI V80.4 — World-Synced Atmosphere & Lighting.
 * A camera-independent 2.5D reconstruction from the NPC's REAR world footprint.
 * We read known room items/buildings and map collision, never the player's viewport.
 * 3D heights/facades are artistic extrapolations, NOT authoritative 3D map meshes.
 * Lighting and weather match the game time + the on-screen V8 weather controller.
 * No gameplay, quest, NPC or save mutation. */
var LQ801Backdrop = (() => {
  'use strict';
  const RATIO=10/7, TILE=32;
  const DIRS=Object.freeze({down:{x:0,y:-1},up:{x:0,y:1},left:{x:1,y:0},right:{x:-1,y:0}});
  const active=new Map(), lastDirections=new Map();
  const finite=n=>typeof n==='number'&&Number.isFinite(n);
  const clamp=(n,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,n));
  const overlap=(a,b)=>!!a&&!!b&&a.x<b.x+b.w&&a.x+a.w>b.x&&a.y<b.y+b.h&&a.y+a.h>b.y;
  const rect=(x,y,w,h)=>({x,y,w,h});
  const sceneKey=sc=>[sc?.area?.id||sc?.area?.name||'unknown',sc?.inside?.b?.name||'outdoors',sc?.inside?.floor??'ground'].join('|');
  const hash=s=>{let h=2166136261;for(const c of String(s)){h=Math.imul(h^c.charCodeAt(0),16777619)}return h>>>0;};
  const hex=n=>{const s=n.toString(16).padStart(6,'0');return '#'+s.slice(-6)};
  function tint(color,m=1){const c=color.replace('#','');let v=parseInt(c,16);if(!Number.isFinite(v))return color;return '#'+[16,8,0].map(bit=>clamp(Math.round(((v>>bit)&255)*m),0,255).toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');}
  function direction(v){v=String(v||'').trim().toLowerCase();return ({south:'down',front:'down',north:'up',back:'up',west:'left',east:'right'}[v]|| (DIRS[v]?v:null));}
  function findActor(sc,id){if(!sc||!id||!Array.isArray(sc.npcs))return null;return sc.npcs.find(a=>a?.def?.id===id&&a.sprite?.visible!==false&&finite(a.sprite?.x)&&finite(a.sprite?.y)&&!!a.inside===!!sc.inside)||null;}
  function resolveDirection(actor,sc,session){if(!actor)return {value:null,source:'unknown'};for(const v of [actor.face?.dir,actor.facing,actor.direction,actor.dir]){const d=direction(v);if(d){lastDirections.set(session,d);return {value:d,source:'npc-state'};}}const tex=String(actor.sprite?.texture?.key||'').match(/_(down|up|left|right)_\d+(?:$|_)/i);if(tex){lastDirections.set(session,tex[1].toLowerCase());return {value:tex[1].toLowerCase(),source:'sprite-texture'};}if(lastDirections.has(session))return {value:lastDirections.get(session),source:'last-observed'};return {value:null,source:'unknown'};}
  function getBounds(sc,actor){if(!sc||!actor)return null;if(sc.inside){const ins=sc.inside,sp=ins.spec,unit=TILE*(ins.k||1);if(!finite(sp?.w)||!finite(sp?.h)||!finite(unit)||unit<=0)return null;const b=rect(20000,0,sp.w*unit,sp.h*unit);if(actor.sprite.x<b.x||actor.sprite.x>b.x+b.w||actor.sprite.y<b.y||actor.sprite.y>b.y+b.h)return null;return {bounds:b,unit,location:String(ins.b?.name||'Interior'),room:sceneKey(sc),scope:'interior'};}const a=sc.area;if(!finite(a?.w)||!finite(a?.h))return null;const b=rect(0,0,a.w*TILE,a.h*TILE);if(actor.sprite.x<b.x||actor.sprite.x>b.x+b.w||actor.sprite.y<b.y||actor.sprite.y>b.y+b.h)return null;return {bounds:b,unit:TILE,location:String(a.name||a.id||'Outdoors'),room:sceneKey(sc),scope:'outdoors'};}
  function rearTarget(point,face,unit,span=15){if(!DIRS[face]||!finite(point?.x)||!finite(point?.y)||!finite(unit))return null;const m=unit*.18,w=unit*(span||15),h=unit*(span||15);switch(face){case'down':return rect(point.x-w/2,point.y-h-m,w,h);case'up':return rect(point.x-w/2,point.y+m,w,h);case'left':return rect(point.x+m,point.y-h/2,w,h);case'right':return rect(point.x-w-m,point.y-h/2,w,h);}}
  function intersect(a,b){if(!a||!b)return null;const x=Math.max(a.x,b.x),y=Math.max(a.y,b.y),r=Math.min(a.x+a.w,b.x+b.w),bottom=Math.min(a.y+a.h,b.y+b.h);return r>x&&bottom>y?rect(x,y,r-x,bottom-y):null;}
  // The game world extent is the hard limit. The player's camera is *never* consulted.
  function chooseRegion(target,bounds){return intersect(target,bounds);}
  function assess(sc,npcId,session){const actor=findActor(sc,npcId);if(!actor)return {ok:false,reason:'NPC không ở trong khu vực hiện tại'};const space=getBounds(sc,actor);if(!space)return {ok:false,reason:'Không xác định được không gian của NPC'};const f=resolveDirection(actor,sc,session);if(!f.value)return {ok:false,reason:'Chưa xác định được hướng quay của NPC'};const region=chooseRegion(rearTarget(actor.sprite,f.value,space.unit,sc.inside?12:18),space.bounds);if(!region||region.w<space.unit*.6||region.h<space.unit*.6)return {ok:false,reason:'Không có vùng hậu cảnh phía sau NPC'};return {ok:true,actor,room:space.room,location:space.location,scope:space.scope,facing:f.value,facingSource:f.source,unit:space.unit,region,bounds:space.bounds};}
  function plane(point,actor,face,unit){const back=DIRS[face],right={x:-back.y,y:back.x},dx=(point.x-actor.sprite.x)/unit,dy=(point.y-actor.sprite.y)/unit;return {depth:dx*back.x+dy*back.y,lateral:dx*right.x+dy*right.y};}
  function projectPoint(point,actor,face,unit,W,H,z=0){const q=plane(point,actor,face,unit),d=Math.max(.15,q.depth),scale=1/(1+d*.21);return {x:W/2+q.lateral*(W/7.6)*scale,y:H*.23+H*.69*scale-z*(H*.205)*scale,scale,depth:q.depth,lateral:q.lateral};}
  // Existing world structures only; appearance is reconstructed, not photo-matched.
  const knownItemTypes=new Set(['desk','table','counter','shelf','bookshelf','bookcase','rack','locker','glasscase','paint','board','anvil','forge','stove','fireplace','altar','plant','window','clock','crate','barrel','goods','glass','chair','bed','schooldesk','computer','terminal','statue','cabinet','menu','lantern','crystal','orePile','orepile','minecart','stairs','partition']);
  function contactPoint(r,actor,face){
    const ax=actor.sprite.x, ay=actor.sprite.y;
    return face==='down'?{x:clamp(ax,r.x,r.x+r.w),y:r.y+r.h}:
      face==='up'?{x:clamp(ax,r.x,r.x+r.w),y:r.y}:
      face==='left'?{x:r.x,y:clamp(ay,r.y,r.y+r.h)}:
      {x:r.x+r.w,y:clamp(ay,r.y,r.y+r.h)};
  }
  function collectObjects(sc,shot){
    const objects=[],unit=shot.unit,windowRect=shot.region,actor=shot.actor;
    function add(r,kind,name,type,source,meta){
      if(!overlap(r,windowRect))return;
      const surface=contactPoint(r,actor,shot.facing);
      const center={x:r.x+r.w/2,y:r.y+r.h/2};
      const rel=plane(surface,actor,shot.facing,unit),mid=plane(center,actor,shot.facing,unit);
      if(mid.depth < -4 || rel.depth>25 || Math.abs(rel.lateral)>13)return;
      objects.push({type,kind,name,rect:r,center,surface,depth:rel.depth,source,meta:meta||{}});
    }
    if(sc.inside){
      for(const item of sc.inside.spec?.items||[]){
        if(!knownItemTypes.has(item.k)||![item.x,item.y,item.w,item.h].every(finite))continue;
        const r=rect(20000+item.x*unit,item.y*unit,item.w*unit,item.h*unit);
        add(r,item.k,item.label||item.k,'interior','room-item',{...item});
      }
    }else{
      for(const b of sc.area?.buildings||[]){
        if(![b.x,b.y,b.w,b.h].every(finite))continue;
        add(rect(b.x*TILE,b.y*TILE,b.w*TILE,b.h*TILE),'building',String(b.name||'Building'),'building','area.buildings',b);
      }
      for(const o of sc.area?.obstacles||[]){
        const k=String(o.kind||o.k||o.type||'').toLowerCase();
        if(!['tree','pine','rock','boulder','lamp','lamppost','statue','fountain','tower','stall','sign','garden','torch','barrel','crate'].includes(k)||![o.x,o.y,o.w,o.h].every(finite))continue;
        add(rect(o.x*TILE,o.y*TILE,o.w*TILE,o.h*TILE),k,String(o.label||k),'terrain','area.obstacles',o);
      }
    }
    objects.sort((a,b)=>b.depth-a.depth || a.rect.x-b.rect.x);
    return objects.slice(0,32);
  }
  const styles={
    'english-town':{sky:'#344e65',haze:'#75949a',ground:'#66726a',line:'#889181',wall:'#9b8c71',accent:'#d6b57d',roof:'#77554e',leaf:'#668d7d'},
    'mining-town':{sky:'#384756',haze:'#7c8291',ground:'#686b69',line:'#96948a',wall:'#878786',accent:'#dac69a',roof:'#55585e',leaf:'#708778'},
    'tech-co':{sky:'#1a4157',haze:'#477b88',ground:'#506e7b',line:'#7897a2',wall:'#779aa5',accent:'#b3d9d7',roof:'#365568',leaf:'#6b978a'},
    'old-oriaris':{sky:'#514051',haze:'#99818b',ground:'#76686b',line:'#c5a693',wall:'#b09b83',accent:'#e6cc91',roof:'#4d4356',leaf:'#799081'},
    'wish-valley':{sky:'#285367',haze:'#8bb7a3',ground:'#4c7d67',line:'#a4ba87',wall:'#987f5d',accent:'#e0c982',roof:'#426451',leaf:'#56a879'},
    'vocalt-greedy-land':{sky:'#493e4b',haze:'#bd9274',ground:'#816f63',line:'#c6aa85',wall:'#b08a68',accent:'#f1d38d',roof:'#704b50',leaf:'#798b65'},
    'art-district':{sky:'#5b4773',haze:'#a785a9',ground:'#7d6b81',line:'#bfa0a9',wall:'#b49ca1',accent:'#ffe5a1',roof:'#665477',leaf:'#829b83'},
    'english-town-past':{sky:'#5a4c55',haze:'#a18b85',ground:'#8c7e68',line:'#b3a087',wall:'#b3a18b',accent:'#f1d49e',roof:'#72595c',leaf:'#8b9980'}
  };
  function palette(sc){const id=String(sc?.area?.id||'').toLowerCase(),theme=String(sc?.area?.theme||'').toLowerCase(),room=String(sc?.inside?.b?.name||'').toLowerCase();
    const base=styles[id]||(/art/.test(id+' '+theme)?styles['art-district']:/oriaris|royal|past/.test(id+' '+theme)?styles['old-oriaris']:styles['english-town']);
    if(!room)return base;
    // Decor colors adapt only to a known building; never pick an unrelated building image.
    if(/post|mail/.test(room))return {...base,wall:'#a39276',ground:'#71685b',roof:'#745747',accent:'#ecd0a1'};
    if(/cafe|coffee|bakery|restaurant/.test(room))return {...base,wall:'#a5836e',ground:'#796559',roof:'#754e49',accent:'#f4c691'};
    if(/library|book|archive/.test(room))return {...base,wall:'#859488',ground:'#596e65',roof:'#56695d',accent:'#d7c99b'};
    if(/office|tech|lab|computer/.test(room))return {...base,wall:'#668c99',ground:'#485e6b',roof:'#385b70',accent:'#a8d9e0'};
    if(/art|studio|gallery/.test(room))return {...base,wall:'#a48c91',ground:'#786f87',roof:'#786279',accent:'#f5d7a6'};
    if(/shrine|temple|waterfall|elder/.test(room))return {...base,wall:'#8e987b',ground:'#5e7b67',roof:'#58775b',accent:'#edd5a3'};
    return base;
  }
  function fill(g,c,x,y,w,h){g.fillStyle=c;g.fillRect(Math.round(x),Math.round(y),Math.max(0,Math.ceil(w)),Math.max(0,Math.ceil(h)));}
  function path(g,c,points){g.fillStyle=c;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(points[0][0],points[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++)g.lineTo(points[i][0],points[i][1]);g.closePath();g.fill();}
  function stroke(g,c,points,width=1){g.strokeStyle=c;g.lineWidth=width;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(points[0][0],points[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++)g.lineTo(points[i][0],points[i][1]);g.stroke();}
  function rounded(g,color,x,y,w,h,r=2){g.fillStyle=color;g.beginPath();g.roundRect(x,y,w,h,r);g.fill();}
  function grad(g,a,b,W,H){const v=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);v.addColorStop(0,a);v.addColorStop(1,b);g.fillStyle=v;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);}
  function containsWorld(r,x,y){return !!r&&finite(r.x)&&finite(r.y)&&finite(r.w)&&finite(r.h)&&x>=r.x&&y>=r.y&&x<r.x+r.w&&y<r.y+r.h;}
  function floorKind(sc,worldX,worldY){
    const unit=sc?.inside?TILE*(sc.inside.k||1):TILE;
    const tx=sc?.inside?(worldX-20000)/unit:worldX/unit,ty=worldY/unit;
    if(sc?.inside){
      const spec=sc.inside.spec;
      if(!spec||tx<0||ty<0||tx>=spec.w||ty>=spec.h)return 'void';
      for(const it of spec.items||[]){
        if(!['rug','mat','carpet','garden','water','pool','pond'].includes(String(it.k||'')))continue;
        if(containsWorld(it,tx,ty))return /water|pool|pond/.test(it.k)?'water':/garden/.test(it.k)?'grass':'rug';
      }
      return 'indoor';
    }
    const a=sc?.area;if(!a||tx<0||ty<0||tx>=a.w||ty>=a.h)return 'void';
    if(containsWorld(a.lake,tx,ty))return 'water';
    if(Array.isArray(a.ponds)&&a.ponds.some(r=>containsWorld(r,tx,ty)))return 'water';
    const roadH=(a.roadsH||[]).some(r=>tx>=(r.x0??0)&&tx<(r.x1??a.w)&&ty>=r.y&&ty<r.y+r.h);
    const roadV=(a.roadsV||[]).some(r=>ty>=(r.y0??0)&&ty<(r.y1??a.h)&&tx>=r.x&&tx<r.x+r.w);
    if(roadH||roadV)return 'road';
    if(containsWorld(a.park,tx,ty)||containsWorld(a.garden,tx,ty))return 'grass';
    if(containsWorld(a.campus,tx,ty))return 'courtyard';
    return 'terrain';
  }
  function weatherState(sc){
    // The V8 weather controller owns the in-game weather display. It selects
    // rain / mist / clear by the game DAY and labels #v8-weather-condition.
    // It does NOT normally write to sc.store.game.weather. Check its label
    // before optional alternate game-state weather to match what the player sees.
    let label='';
    if(typeof document!=='undefined'){
      const e=document.getElementById('v8-weather-condition');
      if(e) label=String(e.textContent||'').trim().toLowerCase();
    }
    const state=String(sc?.store?.game?.weather??sc?.weather?.kind??sc?.weather?.type??'').trim().toLowerCase();
    function parse(v){
      if(/\brain|mưa|mua|storm|giông|wet\b/.test(v))return 'rain';
      if(/\bmist|\bfog|sương|suong/.test(v))return 'mist';
      if(/\bclear|\bsunny|\bfair|quang|nắng|nang/.test(v))return 'clear';
      return null;
    }
    const fromUi=parse(label),fromStore=parse(state);
    if(fromUi)return {type:fromUi,source:'v8-weather-display',label};
    if(fromStore)return {type:fromStore,source:'game-state',label:state};
    return {type:'unknown',source:'unavailable',label:''};
  }
  function mixColor(a,b,t){
    if(!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(a)||!/^#[0-9a-f]{6}$/i.test(b))return a;
    const aa=parseInt(a.slice(1),16),bb=parseInt(b.slice(1),16),r=clamp(t,0,1);
    const chans=[16,8,0].map(shift=>Math.round(((aa>>shift)&255)*(1-r)+((bb>>shift)&255)*r));
    return '#'+chans.map(n=>n.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
  }
  function lightState(sc,shot){
    const minutes=sc?.store?.game?.minutes;
    const known=finite(minutes),clock=known?((minutes%1440)+1440)%1440:null;
    const hour=clock===null?null:clock/60;
    const external=weatherState(sc);
    const rainy=external.type==='rain',mist=external.type==='mist',wetness=!sc?.inside&&rainy?1:0;
    const dayShape=known?Math.max(0,Math.sin(Math.PI*clamp((hour-5.2)/(18.8-5.2),0,1))):.64;
    // Civil/ambient twilight fades gradually instead of one-frame jumps at 19:00.
    const ambientDay=known?clamp((dayShape-.005)*1.12+.09,.18,1):.76;
    const twilight=known&&hour>=4.8&&hour<7.1||known&&hour>=16.9&&hour<19.8;
    const night=known&&(hour<5.2||hour>=18.8);
    const dawn=known&&hour>=4.8&&hour<8.0,evening=known&&hour>=16.5&&hour<19.8;
    const dusk=twilight;
    // Smooth solar balance; direct shadow disappears beneath cloud or mist.
    const cloud=rainy?.22:mist?.11:1;
    const direct=dayShape*(night?0:1)*cloud;
    const atmosphere=rainy?.76:mist?.84:1;
    const sunX=!known?0:hour<12?1:-1;
    const sunY=!known?0:hour<12?-.34:.34;
    const back=DIRS[shot.facing]||DIRS.down,right={x:-back.y,y:back.x};
    const relativeX=sunX*right.x+sunY*right.y;
    const local=[];
    if(sc.inside){for(const item of sc.inside.spec?.items||[]){
      const type=String(item.k||'').toLowerCase();
      if(!['lantern','lamp','fireplace','stove','forge','torch','candle','window'].includes(type))continue;
      if(![item.x,item.y,item.w,item.h].every(finite))continue;
      const px=20000+(item.x+item.w*.5)*shot.unit,py=(item.y+item.h*.5)*shot.unit;
      const rel=plane({x:px,y:py},shot.actor,shot.facing,shot.unit);
      if(rel.depth>-.5&&rel.depth<16&&Math.abs(rel.lateral)<12)local.push({type,x:px,y:py,depth:rel.depth,intensity:type==='window'?ambientDay*atmosphere:night?1:.58});
    }}else{
      for(const item of sc.area?.obstacles||[]){
        const type=String(item.kind||item.k||item.type||'').toLowerCase();
        if(!['lamp','lamppost','torch','lantern','fire','fireplace'].includes(type))continue;
        if(![item.x,item.y,item.w,item.h].every(finite))continue;
        const px=(item.x+item.w*.5)*TILE,py=(item.y+item.h*.5)*TILE;
        const rel=plane({x:px,y:py},shot.actor,shot.facing,shot.unit);
        if(rel.depth>-.5&&rel.depth<16&&Math.abs(rel.lateral)<12)local.push({type,x:px,y:py,depth:rel.depth,intensity:night?1:.24});
      }
    }
    const windowLit=local.some(x=>x.type==='window'&&x.intensity>.12);
    const artificial=local.filter(x=>x.type!=='window');
    const diffuse=clamp((sc.inside?.66:.95)*ambientDay*atmosphere+(sc.inside?Math.min(.38,artificial.length*(night?.245:.11)):0),.17,1);
    const solarDirect=sc.inside?(windowLit?direct*.26:0):direct;
    const shadowStrength=clamp(solarDirect*(rainy?.35:mist?.3:.37),0,.42);
    const shadowLength=solarDirect>0?clamp(1.1/(dayShape+.24),.6,2.8):0;
    return {known,hour,night,dusk,dawn,evening,zenith:dayShape,relativeX,diffuse,
      sunX,sunY,solarDirect,shadowStrength,shadowLength,ambientDay,atmosphere,
      rainy,mist,wetness,weather:external.type,weatherSource:external.source,
      weatherLabel:external.label,local,windowLit,ambientSource:known?'game-clock':'unspecified',
      exposure:diffuse,interior:!!sc.inside};
  }
  function groundColor(kind,P,sc,tx,ty,light){
    const variance=((hash(Math.floor(tx*5)+':'+Math.floor(ty*5))%7)-3)*.009;
    const shades={indoor:P.ground,road:sc?.area?.id==='mining-town'?'#777978':tint(P.line,.95),
      water:'#367e96',grass:P.leaf,terrain:P.ground,courtyard:tint(P.wall,.92),
      rug:tint(P.roof,.96),void:P.sky};
    let c=tint(shades[kind]||P.ground,1+variance);
    if(light?.rainy&&!sc?.inside){
      c=mixColor(c,kind==='water'?'#667f8f':'#3c525b',kind==='road'?.30:.19);
    }else if(light?.mist&&!sc?.inside)c=mixColor(c,'#778d93',.12);
    if(light?.night)c=mixColor(c,'#172b40',sc?.inside?.08:.17);
    return c;
  }
  function rearWorldPoint(shot,lat,depth){
    const b=DIRS[shot.facing],right={x:-b.y,y:b.x},a=shot.actor.sprite;
    return {x:a.x+(b.x*depth+right.x*lat)*shot.unit,y:a.y+(b.y*depth+right.y*lat)*shot.unit};
  }
  function drawGround(g,sc,shot,P,W,H,seed,lighting){
    const far=shot.scope==='interior'?13:19,step=1,spread=14;
    for(let d=far;d>=0;d-=step){
      const near=Math.max(.12,d-.01),away=d+step;
      for(let lat=-spread;lat<spread;lat+=step){
        const mid=rearWorldPoint(shot,lat+.5,d+.5);
        const kind=floorKind(sc,mid.x,mid.y);if(kind==='void')continue;
        const A=projectPoint(rearWorldPoint(shot,lat,near),shot.actor,shot.facing,shot.unit,W,H);
        const B=projectPoint(rearWorldPoint(shot,lat+step,near),shot.actor,shot.facing,shot.unit,W,H);
        const C=projectPoint(rearWorldPoint(shot,lat+step,away),shot.actor,shot.facing,shot.unit,W,H);
        const D=projectPoint(rearWorldPoint(shot,lat,away),shot.actor,shot.facing,shot.unit,W,H);
        const c=groundColor(kind,P,sc,mid.x/shot.unit,mid.y/shot.unit,lighting);
        path(g,tint(c,clamp(lighting.diffuse+.19,.34,1.04)),[[A.x,A.y+1],[B.x,B.y+1],[C.x,C.y-1],[D.x,D.y-1]]);
        const nearWidth=Math.abs(B.x-A.x),n=hash(Math.floor(mid.x/shot.unit)+','+Math.floor(mid.y/shot.unit));
        if(nearWidth>18&&kind==='road'&&n%3===0)stroke(g,tint(P.line,lighting.rainy?.64:.72),[[A.x+2,A.y-2],[B.x-2,B.y-2]],.75);
        if(nearWidth>15&&kind==='indoor'&&n%4===0)stroke(g,tint(P.wall,.73),[[A.x+3,A.y-2],[B.x-3,B.y-2]],.7);
        if(nearWidth>16&&kind==='water')stroke(g,lighting.night?'#6891a057':'#a5d5d76b',[[C.x+3,C.y+2],[D.x-3,D.y+2]],1.3);
        // Reflected highlights are material effects on VERIFIED roads/courtyards, not invented ponds.
        if(lighting.wetness&&nearWidth>24&&(kind==='road'||kind==='courtyard')&&n%7===0){
          const x=A.x+nearWidth*.2,y=A.y+(C.y-A.y)*.13;
          stroke(g,'#bdd6da50',[[x,y],[x+nearWidth*.48,y-1]],1.5);
          if(n%2===0)stroke(g,'#7db3c354',[[x+nearWidth*.12,y+3],[x+nearWidth*.36,y+2]],.9);
        }
        if(nearWidth>18&&(kind==='grass'||kind==='terrain')&&n%6===0){
          const mx=(A.x+B.x+C.x+D.x)/4,my=(A.y+B.y+C.y+D.y)/4;
          stroke(g,tint(P.leaf,lighting.night?.70:1.07),[[mx-2,my+2],[mx,my-4],[mx+1,my+2]],1.1);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  function drawBackdrop(g,sc,shot,P,W,H,seed,lighting){
    const l=lighting||lightState(sc,shot),horizon=H*.28;
    const darkSky=mixColor(P.sky,'#0b1930',.64),duskSky=mixColor(P.sky,'#d99a77',.33);
    const cloudSky=mixColor(P.sky,'#8a99a0',l.rainy?.58:l.mist?.40:0);
    const top=l.night?darkSky:l.dusk&&!l.rainy&&!l.mist?duskSky:cloudSky;
    const bottom=l.night?mixColor(P.haze,'#183148',.63):
      l.rainy?mixColor(P.haze,'#8ca3aa',.42):l.mist?mixColor(P.haze,'#acb9b7',.38):
      l.dusk?mixColor(P.haze,'#e9ba8d',.28):P.haze;
    grad(g,top,bottom,W,H);
    if(sc.inside){
      // Interior walls are lit by room ambient and CONFIRMED windows/fixtures.
      const roomColor=tint(P.wall,clamp(l.diffuse+.20,.28,1.03));
      fill(g,roomColor,0,0,W,horizon+10);
      const wood=/post|cafe|library|wish|craft|home/i.test(String(sc.inside?.b?.name||''));
      const panel=Math.max(33,W/Math.max(6,Math.min(14,Math.floor(shot.bounds.w/shot.unit))));
      const lines=tint(P.roof,clamp(l.diffuse+.02,.28,.94));
      if(wood){for(let x=0;x<W;x+=panel){fill(g,lines,x,8,2,horizon);fill(g,tint(P.accent,.59),x+3,12,1,horizon-17);}}
      else{for(let y=24;y<horizon;y+=23){fill(g,lines,0,y,W,1);for(let x=0;x<W;x+=panel)fill(g,tint(P.roof,.65),x+(y%46?panel/2:0),y+2,1,20);}}
      fill(g,tint(P.roof,.62),0,horizon-4,W,6);
    }else{
      // No fake skylines / artificial sunlight behind an unrelated building.
      fill(g,mixColor(bottom,P.ground,.16),0,horizon-3,W,5);
      if(l.solarDirect>.18&&l.dusk){
        const sx=clamp(W*(.5+.33*l.relativeX),W*.12,W*.88),sy=horizon*.63;
        const sun=g.createRadialGradient(sx,sy,2,sx,sy,W*.32);
        sun.addColorStop(0,'#ffdaa23a');sun.addColorStop(.35,'#f8ce8219');sun.addColorStop(1,'#ffffff00');
        g.fillStyle=sun;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H*.62);
      }
      if(l.mist){const haze=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H*.80);haze.addColorStop(0,'#c1d2d118');haze.addColorStop(.42,'#b9c9c735');haze.addColorStop(1,'#b9c9c700');g.fillStyle=haze;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H*.8);}
    }
    fill(g,tint(P.ground,clamp(l.diffuse+.20,.28,.95)),0,horizon,W,H-horizon);
    drawGround(g,sc,shot,P,W,H,seed,l);
    if(l.night){const veil=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);veil.addColorStop(0,'#08192c29');veil.addColorStop(1,'#091a2d1a');g.fillStyle=veil;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);}
  }
  function drawAtmosphere(g,sc,shot,l,W,H,seed){
    if(sc.inside)return;
    if(l.mist){
      const haze=g.createLinearGradient(0,H*.19,0,H*.90);
      haze.addColorStop(0,'#c4d7d61a');haze.addColorStop(.32,'#ced9d83d');haze.addColorStop(1,'#bdcdcb06');
      g.fillStyle=haze;g.fillRect(0,H*.19,W,H*.72);
    }
    if(l.rainy){
      // Same falling-right rain direction as V8 paintWeather (+3,+14).
      // Not drawn indoors, not flashing; deterministic per scene and clock bucket.
      const count=Math.floor(W/18),offset=hash(seed+':rain')%31;
      g.strokeStyle=l.night?'#a1c2d24e':'#b9d9de59';g.lineWidth=1.0;
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const n=hash(seed+':'+i),x=(n%W+offset)%W,y=((n>>>12)%Math.floor(H*.87));
        g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y);g.lineTo(x+3,y+12);g.stroke();
      }
    }
  }
  function prism(g,x,ground,w,height,depth,c,outline,lighting){
    const ww=Math.max(3,w),hh=Math.max(4,height),dd=clamp(depth,2,ww*.34),side=dd*.70;
    const l=lighting||{diffuse:.87,relativeX:.3,zenith:.7,solarDirect:0};
    const f=l.diffuse;
    // Light on top/side/front is related to the SAME solar vector; cloud
    // and fog flatten highlights. No strong indoor sun without real windows.
    const directional=(l.solarDirect||0),fromSide=l.relativeX*directional;
    const sideMul=clamp(f*(.81+.25*fromSide),.21,1.1);
    const frontMul=clamp(f*(.99-.10*fromSide),.24,1.15);
    const roofMul=clamp(f*(1.02+.24*directional),.27,1.21);
    path(g,tint(c,roofMul),[[x-ww/2,ground-hh],[x-ww/2+dd,ground-hh-side],[x+ww/2+dd,ground-hh-side],[x+ww/2,ground-hh]]);
    path(g,tint(c,sideMul),[[x+ww/2,ground-hh],[x+ww/2+dd,ground-hh-side],[x+ww/2+dd,ground-side],[x+ww/2,ground]]);
    fill(g,tint(c,frontMul),x-ww/2,ground-hh,ww,hh);
    stroke(g,outline||tint(c,.52),[[x-ww/2,ground],[x-ww/2,ground-hh],[x+ww/2,ground-hh],[x+ww/2,ground]],1.4);
    return {left:x-ww/2,right:x+ww/2,top:ground-hh,bottom:ground,w:ww,h:hh};
  }
  function material(g,box,sc,obj,P){
    const {left,right,top,bottom,w,h}=box,area=String(sc.area?.id||''),wood=/wish-valley|english-town/.test(area)||sc.inside&&/post|cafe|studio/i.test(sc.inside.b?.name||'');
    // Pixel-textured masonry/wood panels apply ONLY to a geometry item confirmed in game.
    if(w<17||h<14)return;
    const mortar=tint(P.roof,wood ? .89 : .92),stepY=wood?Math.max(8,h/6):Math.max(8,h/9);
    for(let y=top+stepY;y<bottom-4;y+=stepY){fill(g,mortar,left+2,y,w-4,1);
      if(wood){for(let x=left+8;x<right-5;x+=Math.max(16,w/5))fill(g,tint(P.wall,1.13),x,y+2,2,stepY-3);}
      else {const row=Math.floor((y-top)/stepY);for(let x=left+(row%2)*Math.max(11,w/6);x<right-3;x+=Math.max(15,w/5))fill(g,tint(P.wall,.73),x,y+2,1,Math.max(2,stepY-3));}
    }
    // Edge bevels and base molding make an actual raised volume.
    fill(g,tint(P.accent,.76),left+3,top+4,3,Math.max(1,h-7));fill(g,tint(P.roof,.64),right-5,top+4,3,Math.max(1,h-7));
    fill(g,tint(P.roof,.64),left,bottom-5,w,4);
  }
  function architecture(sc,obj){
    const area=String(sc.area?.id||'').toLowerCase(),name=String(obj.name||'').toLowerCase(),k=String(obj.meta?.kind||'').toLowerCase();
    if(/post office|mailroom|post depot|postal/.test(name))return 'postal';
    if(/cafe|coffee|bakery|restaurant|tea house/.test(name))return 'cafe';
    if(/library|bookshop|book store|archive/.test(name))return 'library';
    if(/school|classroom/.test(name)&&area==='english-town')return 'school';
    if(/tech|office|lab|innovation|meeting|headquarter/.test(area+' '+name))return 'tech';
    if(/wish-valley|maorai|root|canopy|wildfruit/.test(area+' '+name))return 'organic';
    if(/old-oriaris|royal|palace|academy|opera|dynasty/.test(area+' '+name))return 'royal';
    if(/vocalt|greedy/.test(area))return /townhome|home/.test(k+' '+name)?'house':/stall|food|fabric|jewelry|book|artisan|exchange|metal|spire|clock/.test(k)?'market':'merchant';
    if(/mining|minerals/.test(area))return 'industrial';
    if(/art|gallery|studio/.test(area+' '+name))return 'creative';
    if(/past/.test(area))return 'historic';
    return 'civic';
  }
  function ornament(g,b,P,obj,arch,l,scale){
    const {left,right,top,bottom,w,h}=b;
    if(w<40||h<29)return;
    const seed=hash(String(obj.name)+'@'+obj.rect.x+':'+obj.rect.y);
    const detail=tint(P.accent,l.night?.55:1.05),dark=tint(P.roof,l.night?.49:.64);
    if(arch==='tech'){
      // Glass mullion reflection / facade vent: only on an existing verified building.
      for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
        const xx=left+8+(i+.5)*(w-16)/9,yy=top+h*.19;
        fill(g,'#d9f5e328',xx,yy,Math.max(1,2*scale),Math.max(2,h*.23));
      }
      fill(g,dark,left+7,bottom-18,w-14,4);
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/16);i++)fill(g,tint(P.accent,.84),left+8+i*16,bottom-17,7,1);
    }else if(arch==='royal'||arch==='historic'){
      // Subtle stone carving and pilaster relief.
      for(let i=0;i<Math.min(10,Math.ceil(w/24));i++){
        const xx=left+8+i*(w-16)/Math.max(1,Math.ceil(w/24));
        fill(g,tint(P.wall,l.night?.55:1.12),xx,top+h*.14,2,h*.09);
        fill(g,dark,xx-3,top+h*.22,8,2);
      }
      for(let x=left+11;x<right-11;x+=27){fill(g,dark,x,bottom-22,14,1);fill(g,detail,x+2,bottom-25,8,1);}
      fill(g,dark,left+6,top+h*.24,w-12,3);
      fill(g,detail,left+6,top+h*.24,w-12,1);
      // A single crest is engraved into existing architecture, not an extra landmark.
      if(w>160){const cx=(left+right)/2,cy=top+h*.18;
        path(g,dark,[[cx-9,cy-5],[cx+9,cy-5],[cx+8,cy+5],[cx,cy+12],[cx-8,cy+5]]);
        path(g,detail,[[cx-5,cy-3],[cx+5,cy-3],[cx+4,cy+3],[cx,cy+8],[cx-4,cy+3]]);
      }
    }else if(arch==='organic'){
      // Distinct Maorai architecture: carved bark, overhanging foliage and braided canopy.
      for(let i=0;i<11;i++){
        const xx=left+10+(i+.5)*(w-20)/11,yy=top+5+(i%3)*3;
        const rr=clamp(w/16,5,18);
        g.fillStyle=tint(P.leaf,i%3===0?.72:i%3===1?.95:1.18);
        g.beginPath();g.ellipse(xx,yy,rr,rr*.6,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
        fill(g,tint(P.leaf,1.27),xx-rr*.32,yy-rr*.2,rr*.3,2);
      }
      for(let k=0;k<6;k++){
        const xx=left+12+k*(w-24)/5;
        stroke(g,dark,[[xx,top+h*.37],[xx-2,top+h*.57],[xx+2,top+h*.72]],Math.max(1,2*scale));
        fill(g,detail,xx,top+h*.58,3,3);
      }
      for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
        const yy=top+h*(.43+i*.065),x=left+11+((i*17+seed)%21);
        stroke(g,tint(P.roof,.68),[[x,yy],[x+w*.23,yy+1]],1.3);
      }
    }else if(arch==='market'||arch==='merchant'){
      // Embossed retail architecture, striping, ornate awning fringe.
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/14);i++){
        const xx=left+5+i*14;
        fill(g,detail,xx,top+h*.15,5,2);
        fill(g,dark,xx+3,bottom-10,5,2);
      }
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/17);i++){
        const xx=left+8+i*17,yy=top+h*.44;
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.58),xx,yy,2,Math.max(3,h*.10));
        path(g,tint(P.accent,i%2===0?.8:1.05),[[xx-2,yy],[xx+5,yy],[xx+2,yy+8*scale]]);
      }
    }else if(arch==='creative'){
      // Paint marks on established Art District structure, not random shops.
      for(let i=0;i<Math.min(8,Math.floor(w/22));i++){
        const x=left+9+(i*23+seed%13)%(w-18),y=top+h*(.18+(i%3)*.035);
        fill(g,[P.haze,P.leaf,P.accent][i%3],x,y,Math.max(3,4*scale),Math.max(2,8*scale));
      }
    }else if(arch==='industrial'){
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/24);i++){
        const xx=left+12+i*24;
        fill(g,dark,xx,top+h*.22,4,4);fill(g,detail,xx+1,top+h*.23,1,1);
      }
    }else{
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/15);i++){
        const x=left+5+i*15,y=top+h*.17+((i+seed)%2)*2;
        fill(g,detail,x,y,7,1);
      }
    }
  }
  function facadeWindow(l,obj,dayColor){
    // A warm window at night requires known source metadata; by default it
    // is an unlit reflective pane. Never illuminate an entire street by fiat.
    const m=obj?.meta||{};
    const lit=m.lightsOn===true||m.lit===true||m.illuminated===true;
    return l.night?(lit?'#c28b58':'#344955'):
      l.rainy?mixColor(dayColor,'#728a96',.35):
      l.mist?mixColor(dayColor,'#9fb0b1',.22):dayColor;
  }
  function facadeFinish(g,b,obj,arch,l,P){
    // Material-only details on the confirmed building footprint. No extra
    // houses, shop interiors or streetlights are instantiated.
    const {left,right,top,bottom,w,h}=b;
    if(w<40||h<35)return;
    g.save();g.beginPath();g.rect(left+3,top+8,Math.max(0,w-6),Math.max(0,h-15));g.clip();
    const n=hash(obj.name+'|'+Math.floor(obj.rect.x/32)+','+Math.floor(obj.rect.y/32));
    if(arch==='tech'){
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/23);i++){
        const x=left+11+i*23+(n%6);
        stroke(g,l.night?'#9fb9ca10':l.rainy?'#c0dee51b':'#e5f5f727',[[x,top+14],[x+14,top+Math.max(35,h*.46)]],Math.max(1,w*.002));
      }
      fill(g,'#071f2b23',left+4,top+h*.76,w-8,h*.04);
    }else if(arch==='organic'){
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/21);i++){
        const x=left+12+i*21+(n%6),y=top+13+(i*29)%Math.max(14,Math.floor(h*.55));
        stroke(g,'#d1c9a622',[[x,y],[x+7,y+3],[x+18,y+2]],1.0);
      }
    }else if(arch==='royal'||arch==='historic'){
      for(let y=top+20;y<bottom-13;y+=27){
        stroke(g,'#e7d1bc20',[[left+6,y],[right-6,y]],1);
        for(let j=0;j<Math.floor(w/47);j++)fill(g,'#1b192222',left+5+j*47+((y-top)%33),y+2,1,14);
      }
    }else if(arch==='merchant'||arch==='market'){
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/33);i++){
        const x=left+8+i*33;
        fill(g,l.night?'#ac977729':'#f1d59f36',x,top+12,12,2);
        stroke(g,'#0b152327',[[x,top+h*.62],[x+2,top+h*.81]],1);
      }
    }else{
      for(let i=0;i<Math.floor(w/25);i++){
        const x=left+10+i*25,y=top+10+(n+i*19)%Math.max(22,Math.floor(h*.64));
        fill(g,'#dbc9a51b',x,y,9,1);
      }
    }
    if(l.rainy&&!l.interior){
      // Damp tint belongs to the facade only. Geometry/placement unchanged.
      fill(g,'#2d4c5e12',left+2,top+h*.68,w-4,h*.3);
      for(let j=0;j<Math.floor(w/43);j++){
        const x=left+7+j*43+(n%9);stroke(g,'#c7d5d42a',[[x,top+11],[x+1,top+Math.min(h*.32,38)]],.8);
      }
    }
    g.restore();
  }
  function buildingDetails(g,sc,obj,b,P,scale,l,face){
    const {left,right,top,bottom,w,h}=b,name=String(obj.name||''),arch=architecture(sc,obj);
    const facade=face==='down'?'front':face==='up'?'rear':'side';
    const trim=tint(P.accent,l.night?.70:1.02),edge=tint(P.roof,.69);
    // Only the EXISTING footprint is used; architecture and windows are stylization, not measured meshes.
    const cap=Math.max(3,7*scale);
    if(facade==='side'){
      // Looking across a side wall: no entrance signage, shop awnings or front doors.
      const cols=clamp(Math.floor(w/44),1,5);
      for(let i=0;i<cols;i++){const xx=left+(i+.5)*w/cols,yy=top+h*.36;
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.53),xx-8,yy-2,17,Math.max(13,h*.33));
        fill(g,facadeWindow(l,obj,tint(P.haze,.93)),xx-5,yy+1,11,Math.max(7,h*.33-6));
        fill(g,tint(P.accent,.65),xx-10,yy+h*.33,20,2);
      }
      fill(g,tint(P.roof,.73),left+4,bottom-10,w-8,4);
      return;
    }
    if(arch==='tech'){
      fill(g,edge,left,top+4,w,Math.max(2,6*scale));
      const cols=clamp(Math.floor(w/24),2,10),ww=(w-14)/cols;
      for(let j=0;j<cols;j++){
        const x=left+7+j*ww;const y=top+14,hg=Math.max(10,h*.65);
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.56),x,y,ww-3,hg);
        fill(g,facadeWindow(l,obj,'#8dbcc5'),x+3,y+3,Math.max(3,ww-9),Math.max(6,hg-9));
        fill(g,'#d0ece94d',x+5,y+5,2,Math.max(2,hg-13));
        if(hg>35)fill(g,edge,x,y+hg*.52,ww-3,2);
      }
      fill(g,trim,left+2,bottom-10,w-4,3);
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.57),[[left-2,top+4],[left+8,top-cap],[right+8,top-cap],[right+2,top+4]]);
    } else if(arch==='postal'){
      // Postal architecture with recessed letterboxes, engraved envelope icon and arched doorway.
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.66),[[left-10,top+6],[left+11,top-22*scale],[right-11,top-22*scale],[right+10,top+6]]);
      fill(g,trim,left+7,top+9,w-14,4);
      for(let i=0;i<Math.min(9,Math.floor(w/27));i++){
        const xx=left+12+i*(w-24)/Math.min(9,Math.floor(w/27)),yy=top+h*.39;
        fill(g,edge,xx,yy,Math.max(11,w/16),Math.max(12,h*.20));
        fill(g,tint(P.accent,.86),xx+2,yy+3,Math.max(7,w/16-4),Math.max(6,h*.20-6));
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.69),xx+3,yy+5,Math.max(5,w/16-6),1);
      }
      const cx=(left+right)/2,cy=top+h*.18,ww=clamp(w*.12,18,50);
      fill(g,tint(P.accent,.92),cx-ww/2,cy,ww,ww*.57);
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.74),[[cx-ww/2,cy],[cx,cy+ww*.30],[cx+ww/2,cy],[cx+ww/2,cy+ww*.57],[cx-ww/2,cy+ww*.57]]);
      fill(g,trim,left+5,bottom-11,w-10,4);
    } else if(arch==='cafe'){
      // Café front: window bays and a light striped canopy, not the generic civic facade.
      const ay=top+h*.43;
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.76),[[left-7,ay-19*scale],[right+7,ay-19*scale],[right+12,ay+8*scale],[left-12,ay+8*scale]]);
      const stripeW=w/12;
      for(let i=0;i<12;i++)fill(g,i%2?tint(P.accent,.86):tint(P.roof,1.14),left+i*stripeW,ay,stripeW,Math.max(4,7*scale));
      for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const xx=left+(i+.55)*w/3,y=top+h*.55,winW=w/5;
        fill(g,edge,xx-winW*.5-3,y-3,winW+6,h*.28+6);
        fill(g,facadeWindow(l,obj,'#a5bdac'),xx-winW*.5,y,winW,h*.28);
        fill(g,tint(P.accent,.80),xx-winW*.5+3,y+h*.23,winW-6,3);
      }
      fill(g,tint(P.accent,.73),left+4,bottom-11,w-8,4);
    } else if(arch==='library'){
      const n=clamp(Math.floor(w/36),2,8),span=w/n;
      for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const xx=left+(i+.5)*span,wy=top+h*.34;
        fill(g,edge,xx-span*.30,wy,span*.6,h*.43);
        fill(g,l.night?'#393b38':'#897e65',xx-span*.25,wy+4,span*.5,h*.36);
        for(let z=0;z<4;z++){
          fill(g,tint(P.roof,.62),xx-span*.22,wy+5+z*h*.078,span*.44,2);
          for(let k=0;k<4;k++)fill(g,[P.accent,P.haze,P.wall][(i+z+k)%3],xx-span*.19+k*span*.10,wy+6+z*h*.078,Math.max(2,span*.06),Math.max(2,h*.06));
        }
      }
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.69),[[left-7,top+4],[left+9,top-17*scale],[right-9,top-17*scale],[right+7,top+4]]);
    } else if(arch==='school'){
      const n=clamp(Math.floor(w/52),2,6),cw=w/n;
      for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
        const xx=left+(i+.5)*cw,wy=top+h*.35;
        fill(g,edge,xx-15,wy-2,30,Math.max(23,h*.38));fill(g,facadeWindow(l,obj,'#98bab4'),xx-12,wy+1,24,Math.max(17,h*.38-6));
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.73),xx,wy,2,h*.36);
      }
      fill(g,trim,left+4,top+h*.19,w-8,3);
    } else if(arch==='organic'){
      fill(g,tint(P.roof,.62),left+8,top+5,5,h-12);fill(g,tint(P.roof,.62),right-13,top+5,5,h-12);
      for(let k=0;k<6;k++){const dx=left+12+k*(w-24)/5;stroke(g,tint(P.leaf,.76),[[dx,top+5],[dx+4,top+12],[dx+((k%2)?-3:5),top+25]],Math.max(1,2*scale));}
      path(g,tint(P.leaf,.64),[[left-21,top+16],[left+2,top-31*scale],[left+w*.43,top-42*scale],[right+21,top+13],[right-10,top+8],[left+8,top+10]]);
      stroke(g,tint(P.accent,.7),[[left+8,top+14],[right-8,top+14]],2);
      if(w>46){fill(g,'#29453b',left+w*.45,bottom-h*.42,w*.19,h*.4);stroke(g,trim,[[left+w*.45,bottom],[left+w*.45,bottom-h*.42],[left+w*.64,bottom-h*.42],[left+w*.64,bottom]],1.8);}
    } else if(arch==='royal'||arch==='historic'){
      fill(g,tint(P.roof,.74),left,top,w,Math.max(5,7*scale));
      const cols=clamp(Math.floor(w/56),2,7),gap=w/cols;
      for(let j=0;j<cols;j++){
        const cx=left+(j+.5)*gap,ww=clamp(gap*.37,10,26),y=top+h*.27,wh=Math.min(h*.31,39);
        path(g,edge,[[cx-ww*.6,y+wh],[cx-ww*.6,y+ww*.48],[cx,y-ww*.24],[cx+ww*.6,y+ww*.48],[cx+ww*.6,y+wh]]);
        fill(g,facadeWindow(l,obj,'#8ea8a9'),cx-ww*.40,y+ww*.48,ww*.8,Math.max(4,wh-ww*.55));
        fill(g,trim,cx-ww*.52,y+wh,ww*1.04,Math.max(2,3*scale));
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.79),cx-ww*.64,bottom-13,Math.max(2,3*scale),13);
      }
      const topY=top-32*scale;
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.71),[[left-7,top+4],[left+w*.1,topY],[right-w*.1,topY],[right+7,top+4]]);
      fill(g,trim,left+4,bottom-10,w-8,3);
    } else if(arch==='market'||arch==='merchant'){
      const canopyY=top+h*.36;
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.72),[[left+4,canopyY-27*scale],[right-4,canopyY-27*scale],[right+5,canopyY+12*scale],[left-5,canopyY+12*scale]]);
      for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const x=left+i*w/8;fill(g,i%2?tint(P.accent,.82):tint(P.roof,1.13),x,canopyY,w/8,Math.max(2,5*scale));}
      const cols=clamp(Math.floor(w/45),1,6),ww=w/(cols+1.4);
      for(let i=0;i<cols;i++){const x=left+(i+.7)*ww,wy=top+h*.53,wh=h*.29;
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.6),x,wy,ww*.62,wh);fill(g,facadeWindow(l,obj,'#c1ae84'),x+3,wy+3,ww*.62-6,wh-6);
        if(wh>16)fill(g,trim,x+4,wy+wh*.54,ww*.62-8,2);
      }
      fill(g,trim,left+3,bottom-9,w-6,3);
    } else if(arch==='industrial'){
      fill(g,tint(P.roof,.61),left+2,top+8,w-4,5);
      const n=clamp(Math.floor(w/34),2,9);for(let j=0;j<n;j++){
        const x=left+(j+.5)*w/n,y=top+h*.39,r=clamp(w/n*.19,5,12);
        g.fillStyle=l.night?'#506375':'#a7acaa';g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
        stroke(g,edge,[[x-r,y],[x+r,y]],1);
      }
      for(let j=0;j<3;j++)fill(g,edge,left+4,top+h*(.60+j*.11),w-8,Math.max(2,2*scale));
    } else if(arch==='creative'){
      const n=clamp(Math.floor(w/42),2,6);for(let j=0;j<n;j++){
        const x=left+(j+.5)*w/n,y=top+h*.37;
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.48),x-9,y-12,18,24);
        fill(g,[P.accent,P.leaf,P.haze][j%3],x-6,y-9,12,18);
        stroke(g,trim,[[x-10,y+14],[x+10,y+14]],1.5);
      }
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.68),[[left-7,top+5],[left+8,top-10*scale],[right-8,top-10*scale],[right+7,top+5]]);
    } else {
      // Civic & residential: restrained roof and timberwork, not generic tiled skyscraper.
      const cols=clamp(Math.floor(w/55),1,5);for(let i=0;i<cols;i++){
        const x=left+(i+.5)*w/cols,y=top+h*.41;
        fill(g,edge,x-11,y-4,22,27);fill(g,facadeWindow(l,obj,'#9ebbb2'),x-8,y-1,16,21);
        fill(g,tint(P.accent,.77),x-12,y+25,24,3);
      }
      path(g,tint(P.roof,.65),[[left-10,top+6],[left+10,top-12*scale],[right-10,top-12*scale],[right+10,top+6]]);
      fill(g,trim,left+3,bottom-9,w-6,3);
    }
    ornament(g,b,P,obj,arch,l,scale);
    // Sign ONLY if a name is in game data; no fabricated local business names.
    if(facade==='front'&&name&&w>75&&h>48){const size=Math.round(clamp(12*scale+7,10,16));g.font=`bold ${size}px system-ui`;
      const str=name.replace(/\s+/g,' ').slice(0,27),tw=g.measureText(str).width+19;
      const ww=Math.min(w-9,Math.max(30,tw)),y=top+Math.max(8,h*.14);
      rounded(g,tint(P.roof,.51),left+w/2-ww/2,y,ww,size+9,3);
      g.fillStyle='#f2e4cb';g.textAlign='center';g.fillText(str,left+w/2,y+size+2,ww-8);
    }
  }
  function shelfDetails(g,obj,b,P,sc,scale){
    const room=String(sc?.inside?.b?.name||'').toLowerCase(),postal=/post|mail/.test(room),office=/office|tech|computer/.test(room),art=/art|studio|gallery/.test(room);
    for(let a=1;a<4;a++){const sy=b.top+b.h*a/4,shelfH=Math.max(5,8*scale);
      fill(g,tint(P.roof,.52),b.left+3,sy,b.w-6,Math.max(2,2*scale));
      if(b.w>28)for(let j=0;j<Math.floor(b.w/Math.max(9,11*scale));j++){
        const x=b.left+6+j*Math.max(9,11*scale),n=hash(obj.center.x+'_'+obj.center.y+'_'+a+'_'+j),w=Math.max(3,5*scale);
        if(postal){
          fill(g,'#392c27',x-1,sy-shelfH-1,w+2,shelfH+2);
          fill(g,'#d2b185',x+1,sy-shelfH+2,Math.max(2,w-2),Math.max(2,shelfH-4));
          fill(g,'#f1dfbd',x+1,sy-shelfH+2,Math.max(2,w-2),Math.max(1,shelfH*.27));
          fill(g,'#98735b',x+2,sy-shelfH+2,Math.max(1,w-4),1);
          fill(g,tint(P.accent,.82),x+2,sy-2,Math.max(2,w-4),1);
        }
        else if(office){fill(g,[P.haze,P.roof,P.accent][n%3],x,sy-shelfH,w,shelfH-1);fill(g,tint(P.accent,.86),x+2,sy-shelfH+2,2,1);}
        else if(art){fill(g,[P.accent,'#bd8a82','#79b3a5'][n%3],x,sy-shelfH,w,shelfH-1);}
        else{fill(g,[P.accent,P.haze,P.roof][n%3],x,sy-shelfH,w,shelfH-1);fill(g,tint(P.wall,1.14),x+1,sy-shelfH+2,1,Math.max(2,shelfH-5));}
      }
    }
  }
  function drawStructure(g,sc,shot,P,W,H,obj,index,lighting){
    const {x,y,w,h}=obj.rect,contact=obj.surface||{x:x+w/2,y:y+h/2};
    const center={x:x+w/2,y:y+h/2};
    // Project width from the ACTUAL center of the facade, not a closest-point snap to the NPC.
    const px=(shot.facing==='down'||shot.facing==='up')?{x:center.x,y:contact.y}:{x:contact.x,y:center.y};
    const pos=projectPoint(px,shot.actor,shot.facing,shot.unit,W,H);
    if(pos.depth<-.85||pos.depth>25)return;
    const scale=pos.scale,wide=(shot.facing==='down'||shot.facing==='up'?w:h)/shot.unit;
    const visualW=clamp(wide*(W/7.9)*scale,10,W*1.30);
    const dim={shelf:2.2,bookshelf:2.3,bookcase:2.3,locker:2.1,rack:1.85,board:1.4,clock:1.1,
      statue:2.1,plant:1.5,forge:1.1,altar:1.2,window:1.5,stairs:1.0,counter:1.0,table:.95,
      desk:1.0,computer:1.25,terminal:1.22,glasscase:1.25,crate:.75,barrel:.9,chair:.8,bed:.65,
      tree:3.1,pine:3.3,rock:.7,boulder:1,lamppost:2.5,lamp:2.5,torch:1.45,fountain:1.4,
      stall:1.3,sign:1.15,garden:.15,tower:3.5}[obj.kind];
    const ar=obj.type==='building'?architecture(sc,obj):'';
    const hTiles=obj.type==='building'?({tech:4.6,royal:5.1,historic:4.1,organic:3.7,market:3.6,merchant:3.7,postal:3.6,cafe:3.45,library:4.0,school:3.9,industrial:3.7,creative:3.5,house:2.9}[ar]||3.5):(dim??1.1);
    let height=clamp(hTiles*H*.215*scale,6,H*.82),floor=pos.y;
    if(obj.type==='building'&&wide>6)height=Math.min(height*1.13,H*.81);
    const light=lighting||lightState(sc,shot);
    // Ground-contact shadow always exists; CAST shadow only with verified sun.
    const sunShadow=light.shadowStrength||0;
    const shift=-(light.relativeX||0)*height*(light.shadowLength||0)*.26;
    const dy=2.6+scale*2.8;
    g.fillStyle=light.night?'#03111e38':'#06192228';g.beginPath();
    g.ellipse(pos.x,floor+dy,Math.max(7,visualW*.45),Math.max(2,4.7*scale),0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
    if(sunShadow>.02){
      const cast=clamp(Math.abs(shift)+height*.15,5,Math.min(W*.36,height*.9));
      const signed=(shift>=0?1:-1);
      const x2=pos.x+signed*cast,y2=floor+Math.max(2,cast*.17);
      path(g,`rgba(8,22,30,${sunShadow.toFixed(3)})`,[
        [pos.x-visualW*.39,floor],[pos.x+visualW*.39,floor],
        [x2+visualW*.20,y2+3],[x2-visualW*.20,y2+3]]);
    }
    if(['tree','pine'].includes(obj.kind)){
      const trunk=Math.max(4,visualW*.13),cx=pos.x;
      fill(g,tint(P.roof,.72),cx-trunk/2,floor-height*.62,trunk,height*.62);
      for(let j=0;j<3;j++){
        const yy=floor-height*(.80-j*.19),rad=Math.max(8,visualW*(.41-j*.02));
        g.fillStyle=tint(P.leaf,light.night?.53:1.1-j*.08);g.beginPath();g.ellipse(cx+(j%2?2:-2)*scale,yy,rad,Math.max(9,height*.26),0,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
        fill(g,tint(P.leaf,.69),cx-rad*.46,yy+height*.02,rad*.55,2);
      }return;
    }
    if(['rock','boulder','crystal','orepile'].includes(obj.kind)){
      path(g,tint(obj.kind==='crystal'?P.accent:P.wall,light.night?.65:.83),[[pos.x-visualW*.42,floor],[pos.x-visualW*.43,floor-height*.38],[pos.x-visualW*.12,floor-height],[pos.x+visualW*.29,floor-height*.72],[pos.x+visualW*.47,floor-height*.23],[pos.x+visualW*.31,floor]]);
      path(g,tint(P.accent,light.night?.63:1.08),[[pos.x-visualW*.12,floor-height],[pos.x+visualW*.06,floor-height*.72],[pos.x-visualW*.20,floor-height*.32]]);return;
    }
    const base=obj.type==='building'?P.wall:obj.kind==='plant'?P.leaf:
      /forge|anvil/.test(obj.kind)?tint(P.wall,.70):/computer|terminal/.test(obj.kind)?tint(P.roof,.85):
      obj.kind==='glasscase'?P.haze:obj.kind==='altar'?tint(P.accent,.82):tint(P.wall,.89);
    const b=prism(g,pos.x,floor,visualW,height,(obj.type==='building'?46:22)*scale,base,tint(P.roof,.55),light);
    if(obj.type==='building'){
      if(!['tech','organic','market'].includes(architecture(sc,obj)))material(g,b,sc,obj,P);
      buildingDetails(g,sc,obj,b,P,scale,light,shot.facing);
      facadeFinish(g,b,obj,ar,light,P);
    } else {
      const k=String(obj.kind).toLowerCase();
      if(/shelf|rack|bookcase|locker|cabinet/.test(k)){shelfDetails(g,obj,b,P,sc,scale);}
      else if(/counter|desk|table|schooldesk|glasscase|goods/.test(k)){
        fill(g,tint(P.accent,light.night?.6:1.04),b.left+2,b.top+2,b.w-4,Math.max(3,5*scale));
        for(let a=1;a<=2;a++)fill(g,tint(P.roof,.58),b.left+b.w*a/3-2,b.top+b.h*.50,3,Math.max(2,b.h*.32));
        if(/post|mail/i.test(String(sc.inside?.b?.name||''))&&b.w>58){
          for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const xx=b.left+9+i*(b.w-18)/3;
            fill(g,tint(P.roof,.66),xx,b.top+b.h*.69,Math.max(8,b.w*.19),3);
            fill(g,tint(P.accent,1.12),xx+3,b.top+b.h*.70,Math.max(3,b.w*.12),1);
          }
          for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const xx=b.left+b.w*.30+i*7;fill(g,'#e7cfab',xx,b.top+2-i,14,2);}
        }
      }
      else if(/board|menu|window|computer|terminal/.test(k)){
        const m=Math.min(b.w*.15,10);fill(g,'#162e37',b.left+m,b.top+6,b.w-2*m,Math.max(8,b.h*.48));
        if(k==='window'){fill(g,light.night?'#405c65':'#93c6cb',b.left+m+3,b.top+9,b.w-2*m-6,Math.max(3,b.h*.48-6));}
        else if(k==='board'||k==='menu'){for(let a=0;a<3;a++)fill(g,tint(P.accent,.88),b.left+m+5,b.top+10+a*7,Math.max(3,b.w-2*m-12),1);}
        else fill(g,tint(P.haze,.91),b.left+m+4,b.top+9,Math.max(3,b.w-2*m-8),Math.max(3,b.h*.27));
      }
      else if(/forge|stove|fireplace|lantern|torch/.test(k)){
        fill(g,tint(P.roof,.56),pos.x-8*scale,b.top+b.h*.26,16*scale,b.h*.34);
        fill(g,'#f0a15d',pos.x-4*scale,b.top+b.h*.28,8*scale,Math.max(3,b.h*.23));
      }
      else if(k==='plant'){g.fillStyle=tint(P.leaf,light.night?.63:1.16);g.beginPath();g.ellipse(pos.x,b.top+b.h*.29,Math.max(7,b.w*.45),Math.max(9,b.h*.35),0,0,2*Math.PI);g.fill();fill(g,tint(P.roof,.72),pos.x-2,b.top+b.h*.42,4,b.h*.43);}
      else if(k==='clock'){g.fillStyle=tint(P.accent,.92);g.beginPath();g.arc(pos.x,b.top+b.h*.3,Math.max(5,Math.min(b.w*.33,b.h*.24)),0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();stroke(g,tint(P.roof,.63),[[pos.x,b.top+b.h*.3],[pos.x,b.top+b.h*.22],[pos.x+b.w*.08,b.top+b.h*.3]],2);}
      else if(k==='crate'||k==='barrel'){stroke(g,tint(P.roof,.54),[[b.left+3,b.top+3],[b.right-3,b.bottom-3]],2);stroke(g,tint(P.roof,.54),[[b.right-3,b.top+3],[b.left+3,b.bottom-3]],2);}
      else{fill(g,tint(P.accent,.8),b.left+4,b.top+4,Math.max(0,b.w-8),Math.max(1,Math.min(3,b.h*.14)));}
    }
  }
  function drawWorld(g,sc,shot,P,W,H){
    const seed=[shot.room,Math.floor(shot.actor.sprite.x/shot.unit),Math.floor(shot.actor.sprite.y/shot.unit),shot.facing].join(':');
    const l=lightState(sc,shot),objects=collectObjects(sc,shot);
    drawBackdrop(g,sc,shot,P,W,H,seed,l);
    for(const obj of objects)drawStructure(g,sc,shot,P,W,H,obj,0,l);
    // Light emitters must originate from actual room items or outdoor fixtures.
    for(const light of l.local){
      if(light.type==='window'&&(l.night||l.solarDirect<=.05))continue;
      const v=projectPoint({x:light.x,y:light.y},shot.actor,shot.facing,shot.unit,W,H);
      const radius=Math.max(20,155*v.scale)*(light.type==='window'?1.12:1);
      const centreY=v.y-35*v.scale;
      const radial=g.createRadialGradient(v.x,centreY,0,v.x,centreY,radius);
      const isWindow=light.type==='window';
      const opacity=isWindow?Math.round(clamp(light.intensity*.16,0,.19)*255):Math.round(clamp(light.intensity*.40,0,.43)*255);
      const alpha=opacity.toString(16).padStart(2,'0');
      radial.addColorStop(0,(isWindow?'#f5e7c4':'#efaa68')+alpha);
      radial.addColorStop(1,'#ffffff00');
      g.fillStyle=radial;g.fillRect(v.x-radius,centreY-radius,radius*2,radius*2);
    }
    drawAtmosphere(g,sc,shot,l,W,H,seed);
    const vignette=g.createRadialGradient(W/2,H*.60,H*.15,W/2,H*.52,W*.75);
    vignette.addColorStop(0,'#00000000');vignette.addColorStop(1,l.night?'#05101e64':l.rainy?'#08141a37':'#07152127');
    g.fillStyle=vignette;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    const hour=finite(sc.store?.game?.minutes)?Math.floor(sc.store.game.minutes/60):null;
    return {objects:objects.length,source:objects.length?'world-geometry':'world-materials',
      light:hour===null?'unspecified':l.night?'night':l.dusk?'twilight':'day',lightSource:l.ambientSource,
      weather:l.weather,weatherSource:l.weatherSource,
      lightFixtures:l.local.length,area:sc.area?.id||'unknown',
      groundKinds:[...new Set([floorKind(sc,shot.actor.sprite.x,shot.actor.sprite.y),floorKind(sc,...Object.values(rearWorldPoint(shot,0,5)))])],
      architecture:objects.filter(x=>x.type==='building').map(x=>architecture(sc,x)),
      ambient:l.diffuse,solarDirect:l.solarDirect,shadowStrength:l.shadowStrength,wetness:l.wetness};
  }
  function neutral(canvas,label='SCENE UNAVAILABLE'){if(!canvas)return;canvas.width=480;canvas.height=336;const g=canvas.getContext('2d');if(!g)return;grad(g,'#263c49','#17232f',480,336);for(let i=0;i<13;i++)fill(g,'#334a55',i*40,145,2,170);g.font='600 13px system-ui';g.fillStyle='#cbd7d3';g.textAlign='center';g.fillText(label,240,172);}
  function makeLabel(s,message,ok){if(s.badge)s.badge.textContent=message;if(s.tag){s.tag.textContent=message;s.tag.dataset.available=String(ok);}if(s.canvas?.parentElement)s.canvas.parentElement.dataset.backdropState=ok?'reconstructed':'neutral';}
  function safeClear(s,message){if(s.canvas)neutral(s.canvas);s.lastFrame=null;makeLabel(s,message||'Nền không khả dụng',false);}
  function render(s){const sc=typeof window!=='undefined'?window.__lifequestGetScene?.():null;if(!s.root.isConnected||!sc||sc!==s.scene||sceneKey(sc)!==s.room){safeClear(s,'Đã chuyển cảnh · cần mở hội thoại mới');return;}
    const shot=assess(sc,s.id,s.token);if(!shot.ok)return safeClear(s,shot.reason);if(active.get(s.canvas)!==s)return;
    const quality=window.LQ79?.quality?.()||'balanced',W=quality==='high'?880:quality==='low'?400:640,H=Math.round(W/RATIO);
    if(s.canvas.width!==W||s.canvas.height!==H){s.canvas.width=W;s.canvas.height=H;}
    const g=s.canvas.getContext('2d');if(!g)return safeClear(s,'Không có Canvas 2D');
    try{g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;const info=drawWorld(g,sc,shot,palette(sc),W,H);s.lastFrame={npcId:s.id,source:'world-synced-atmosphere',confidence:info.objects?'grounded-objects':'location-only',rearWorld:shot.region,location:shot.location,facing:shot.facing,scene:shot.room,objects:info.objects,light:info.light,weather:info.weather,weatherSource:info.weatherSource};
      if(s.canvas?.parentElement){s.canvas.parentElement.dataset.lq804Time=info.light;s.canvas.parentElement.dataset.lq804Weather=info.weather;}
      const hint=shot.facingSource==='last-observed'?' · hướng gần nhất':'';makeLabel(s,`Sau NPC · ${shot.location} · ${info.light==='unspecified'?'giờ chưa rõ':info.light==='night'?'ban đêm':info.light==='twilight'?'bình minh/hoàng hôn':'ban ngày'} · ${info.weatherSource==='unavailable'?'thời tiết chưa rõ':info.weather==='rain'?'mưa nhẹ':info.weather==='mist'?'sương mù':'trời quang'}${hint}`,true);
      if(s.root.id==='chat'){const activity=s.root.querySelector('#lq772-activity'),clock=s.root.querySelector('#lq772-day');if(activity&&shot.actor.slot?.label)activity.textContent=String(shot.actor.slot.label);const day=sc.store?.game?.day,m=sc.store?.game?.minutes;if(clock&&finite(day)&&finite(m))clock.textContent=`DAY ${day} · ${String(Math.floor(m/60)%24).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(Math.floor(m%60)).padStart(2,'0')}`;}}
    catch(e){console.debug('[V80.3] Place-truth rendering fallback:',e);safeClear(s,'Không thể dựng hậu cảnh hiện tại');}
  }
  function detachCanvas(canvas){const s=active.get(canvas);if(!s)return;lastDirections.delete(s.token);s.tag?.remove();active.delete(canvas);if(!active.size&&timer){clearInterval(timer);timer=0;}}
  let timer=0,sequence=0,enabled=true;
  function update(){if(!active.size){if(timer){clearInterval(timer);timer=0;}return;}if(typeof document==='undefined'||document.hidden)return;const now=performance.now();for(const [canvas,s] of active){if(!canvas.isConnected||!s.root.isConnected||s.root.hidden||s.root.closest('[hidden]')||!s.root.getClientRects().length){detachCanvas(canvas);continue;}const sc=window.__lifequestGetScene?.();if(sc!==s.scene||sceneKey(sc)!==s.room){safeClear(s,'Địa điểm đã thay đổi');detachCanvas(canvas);continue;}if(!enabled)continue;const q=window.LQ79?.quality?.()||'balanced',reduced=window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)')?.matches,delay=reduced||q==='low'?4500:q==='high'?950:1800;if(now-s.lastCapture>=delay){s.lastCapture=now;render(s);}}}
  function attach(root,canvas,id,badge){if(!root||!canvas||!id)return;detachCanvas(canvas);const sc=window.__lifequestGetScene?.(),stage=canvas.parentElement;if(!sc){neutral(canvas);if(stage){stage.dataset.backdropState='neutral';let tag=stage.querySelector('.lq802-source');if(!tag){tag=document.createElement('span');tag.className='lq802-source';stage.append(tag);}tag.textContent='Không xác định được cảnh của NPC';tag.dataset.available='false';}if(badge)badge.textContent='Không xác định được cảnh của NPC';return;}const token=`lq802-${++sequence}`;
    if(root.id==='chat'&&stage&&!stage.querySelector('.lq802-focus-btn')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.className='lq802-focus-btn';b.textContent='▣';b.title='Xem rõ hậu cảnh hoặc chân dung';b.setAttribute('aria-label','Đổi cách bố trí sân khấu');b.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');b.addEventListener('click',ev=>{ev.stopPropagation();stage.dataset.sceneFocus=String(stage.dataset.sceneFocus!=='true');b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',stage.dataset.sceneFocus);});stage.append(b);}
    let tag=stage?.querySelector('.lq802-source');if(!tag&&stage){tag=document.createElement('span');tag.className='lq802-source';tag.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');stage.append(tag);}
    const s={root,canvas,id,badge,tag,scene:sc,room:sceneKey(sc),token,lastCapture:0,lastFrame:null};active.set(canvas,s);if(enabled)render(s);else safeClear(s,'Nền đang tắt');if(!timer)timer=setInterval(update,400);
  }
  function detach(root){for(const [canvas,s] of [...active])if(s.root===root)detachCanvas(canvas);}
  function regional(panel,npc,activity){const section=panel?.querySelector('.lq775-regional');if(!section||!npc?.id)return;let bg=section.querySelector('.lq79-regional-bg');if(!bg){bg=document.createElement('canvas');bg.className='lq79-regional-bg';bg.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');section.prepend(bg);}let badge=section.querySelector('.lq79-regional-label');if(!badge){badge=document.createElement('small');badge.className='lq79-regional-label';section.append(badge);}attach(panel,bg,npc.id,badge);const portrait=section.querySelector('canvas:not(.lq79-regional-bg)');if(portrait&&typeof LQ78Motion!=='undefined')LQ78Motion.context(portrait,activity||'');}
  function install(){if(typeof window==='undefined'||!window.LQ79)return;const old=window.LQ79,close=old.close;old.attach=attach;old.detach=detach;old.regional=regional;old.view=function(sc,canvas,id){const x=assess(sc,id,'legacy');return x.ok?x.region:null;};old.close=function(root){detach(root);return close.call(old,root);};}
  const api=Object.freeze({direction,findActor,resolveDirection,getBounds,rearTarget,chooseRegion,assess,plane,projectPoint,collectObjects,contactPoint,floorKind,lightState,architecture,palette,weatherState,drawWorld,attach,detach,regional,currentFrames:()=>[...active.values()].map(s=>s.lastFrame).filter(Boolean),activeCount:()=>active.size,enable(on){enabled=!!on;for(const s of active.values())enabled?render(s):safeClear(s,'Đã tắt nền');}});
  if(typeof window!=='undefined'){window.LQ801Backdrop=api;window.LQ802Backdrop=api;window.LQ803Backdrop=api;window.LQ804Backdrop=api;install();}
  return api;
})();
if(typeof module!=='undefined'&&module.exports)module.exports=LQ801Backdrop;

   All confirmed facts are sourced from the live Phaser scene, game state or
   explicitly completed V80 learning activities. No synthetic facts masquerade
   as observations. DOM output uses textContent only. */
