  function regionOf(sc=scene()){
    if(!sc)return null;
    const area=norm(sc.area?.name||sc.area?.id||''),room=norm(sc.inside?.b?.name||'');
    // Name-based lookup: no invented building ID, deliberately prefer whole-map context.
    return W.find(r=>r.search.test(area))||(!area?W.find(r=>r.search.test(room)):null)||null;
  }
  function place(sc=scene()){return sc?.inside?.b?.name||sc?.area?.name||'Unknown location';}
  function context(npc,sc=scene()){
    const reg=regionOf(sc);const day=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(sc?.store?.game?.day)||1));
    const minutes=clamp(sc?.store?.game?.minutes??480,0,1439);
    const live=(sc?.npcs||[]).find(n=>n.def?.id===npc?.id && n.sprite?.visible && !!n.inside===!!sc.inside);
    const activity=safe(live?.slot?.label||'',120);
    const nearby=global.LQ79?.nearby?.(sc,npc?.id)?.map(n=>({id:n.def.id,name:n.def.name}))||[];
    return {region:reg,day,minutes,place:place(sc),inside:!!sc?.inside,live,activity,nearby,scene:sc};
  }
  function state(chat){
    const game=chat?.store?.game;
    if(!game)return {version:80,world:{},activity:{},investigations:{},ledger:[],contacts:{},links:{},coach:{},settings:{motion:!reduced(),quality:'balanced'}};
    if(!game.lq80||typeof game.lq80!=='object'||Array.isArray(game.lq80))game.lq80={};
    const s=game.lq80;
    for(const k of ['world','activity','investigations','contacts','links','coach','settings'])if(!s[k]||typeof s[k]!=='object'||Array.isArray(s[k]))s[k]={};
    if(!Array.isArray(s.ledger))s.ledger=[];
    if(!['low','balanced','high'].includes(s.settings.quality))s.settings.quality='balanced';
    if(typeof s.settings.motion!=='boolean')s.settings.motion=!reduced();
    s.version=80;return s;
  }
  const persist=chat=>{try{chat.store.save();}catch{}};
  function ledger(chat,kind,label,source){const s=state(chat),c=context(chat.npc);s.ledger.push({kind:safe(kind,32),label:safe(label,180),source:safe(source,55),day:c.day,minutes:c.minutes,region:c.region?.id||null});s.ledger=s.ledger.slice(-90);persist(chat);}
  const styleSignature=(npc)=>{const id=safe(npc?.id||npc?.name||'resident',100);let h=2166136261;for(const char of id)h=Math.imul(h^char.charCodeAt(0),16777619)>>>0;return ['patient','lively','thoughtful','reserved','wry','warm'][h%6]};
  function verifiedInfo(npc,chat){const c=context(npc),s=state(chat),w=s.world[c.region?.id];const nearby=c.nearby.map(n=>n.name).join(', ')||'none confirmed';return `[V80 grounded context] Region: ${c.region?.label||'unavailable'}; observed location: ${c.place}; observed NPC activity: ${c.activity||'not available'}; nearby visible same-room NPCs: ${nearby}. Time: day ${c.day}, ${String(Math.floor(c.minutes/60)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(c.minutes%60).padStart(2,'0')}. V80 completed learning activity: ${w?.completed?c.region?.title+' (tracked by game)':'none'}. Other V80 lore cases are fictional practice exercises, not verified history. NPC's personal response style: ${styleSignature(npc)}. Do not claim an NPC is elsewhere if not observed; do not invent historical facts, store prices, dialogue participants, or completed original missions.`;}
  // Verified local answers take priority over online/offline improvisation for
  // specific world-state questions. This never completes a quest.
  function factualReply(chat,npc,input){
    const q=norm(input).replace(/[?.!]+$/,'');if(!q)return null;
    let intent={};try{intent=typeof LQ78Meaning!=='undefined'?LQ78Meaning.classify(npc,chat.history||[],input):{};}catch{}
    if(intent.injection||intent.quote||intent.hyp||intent.sarcasm)return null;
    const c=context(npc),r=c.region,s=state(chat);
    let reply=null;
    if(/^(?:what (?:has changed|did we change)|what changed|did i (?:finish|complete) (?:the|this) (?:activity|side activity)|have i finished (?:this|the) activity)\b/.test(q)){
      const completed=Object.values(s.world).filter(x=>x?.completed&&x.regionId===r?.id);
      reply=completed.length?`I can confirm these learning activities were recorded here: ${completed.map(x=>x.effect).join('; ')}. That is separate from the original missions.`:'I do not have a completed V80 community activity recorded here yet.';
    }else if(/^(?:what (?:event|events) (?:is|are) (?:happening|going on)|is there (?:a|an) (?:festival|event) (?:now|today)|what is happening around us)\b/.test(q)){
      reply=c.activity?`I can confirm this current activity: ${c.activity}. I cannot confirm a live festival from the game state.`:'I do not have a confirmed live event for this location. The activity schedule in World & learning is an optional exercise, not a report of a real festival.';
    }else if(/^(?:how old are you|what is your age|are you (?:old|young)|what year were you born)\b/.test(q)){
      reply='My exact age is not recorded in the verified NPC profile. My portrait may suggest a life stage, but that is a visual design rather than a confirmed biography.';
    }else if(/^(?:are you (?:friends|related) with|is .* (?:your friend|your family|your relative))\b/.test(q)){
      reply='I cannot confirm that personal relationship from the game records. Being in the same conversation does not prove two people are friends or related.';
    }else if(/^(?:how many (?:people|residents|npcs) are (?:nearby|here)|how many (?:people|residents|npcs) can you see)\b/.test(q)){
      reply=`I can confirm ${c.nearby.length} other visible resident${c.nearby.length===1?'':'s'} close by in this part of the game.`;
    }else if(/^(?:did you (?:see|witness)|can you confirm)\b/.test(q)&&/\b(?:explosion|fire|festival|accident|crime|theft|war)\b/.test(q)){
      reply='I do not have a verified record of that event in the current scene. Could you tell me what evidence you found?';
    }
    return reply===null?null:{reply,goalDone:false,affinityDelta:0,mood:'neutral',replySource:'Verified game state / data limits',topicStatus:'relevant',correction:'',remember:''};
  }
  function sanitizeResponse(npc,input,r){
    if(!r||typeof r!=='object')return r;
    if(r.replySource?.startsWith('Verified context'))return r;
    const prices=typeof lq773ShopFacts==='function'?lq773ShopFacts(npc):[];
    const answer=String(r.reply||'');const text=norm(input+' '+answer);
    for(const item of prices){
      if(!Number.isFinite(item.price))continue;
      const n=norm(item.name).replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\s]/gu,'').trim();
      const w=n.split(/\s+/).filter(x=>x.length>3);if(!w.some(x=>text.includes(x)))continue;
      const amounts=[...answer.matchAll(/(?:\$\s*|\bUSD\s*)(\d+(?:\.\d{1,2})?)/gi)].map(m=>Number(m[1]));
      if(amounts.length&&amounts.some(v=>v!==item.price))return {...r,reply:`The verified shop catalog lists ${item.name} at $${item.price}. Please check the shop before making a purchase.`,goalDone:false,affinityDelta:0,replySource:'Verified in-game shop catalog',notice:'An unverified price was corrected.'};
    }
    return r;
  }
  function initial(chat){const s=state(chat),npc=chat.npc;if(!npc)return;const id=npc.id;if(!s.contacts[id]){s.contacts[id]={met:safe(npc.name,100),firstDay:context(npc).day,turns:0};ledger(chat,'met',`Met ${npc.name}`,'Observed conversation');}persist(chat);}
  function recordTurn(chat,npc,input,r,situation){
    const s=state(chat),contact=s.contacts[npc.id]||(s.contacts[npc.id]={met:npc.name,firstDay:situation.day,turns:0});contact.turns=clamp(contact.turns+1,0,10000);
    const text=norm(input),tone= /\b(sorry|apologize|apologise|forgive me)\b/.test(text)?'apologetic':/\b(thanks|thank you|appreciate)\b/.test(text)?'grateful':/\b(could you|would you|please|may i)\b/.test(text)?'polite':/\b(but|however|on the other hand)\b/.test(text)?'reasoned':'neutral';
    contact.lastTone=tone;contact.lastDay=situation.day;
    if(r.correction&&typeof r.correction==='string'){
      const kind=/\b(was|were|while|when|past)\b/i.test(r.correction)?'past-continuous':/\b(if|wish|would)\b/i.test(r.correction)?'conditionals':/\b(could|please|polite)\b/i.test(r.correction)?'polite-requests':'sentence-clarity';
      const a=s.coach[kind]||(s.coach[kind]={count:0,recent:[]});a.count=Math.min(1000,a.count+1);a.recent=[...a.recent,safe(r.correction,180)].slice(-4);
    }
    ledger(chat,'dialogue',`${npc.name}: ${tone} turn${r.correction?' · teacher feedback':''}`,'Player message / AI feedback');
    if(r.goalDone===true)ledger(chat,'original-quest',`Original mission reported complete: ${safe(npc.goal?.text||'quest',120)}`,'Game mission logic');
    persist(chat);
  }
  function noteGroup(chat,participant){if(!chat?.npc||!participant)return;const s=state(chat),a=String(chat.npc.id),b=String(participant.id||'');if(!b||a===b)return;const k=[a,b].sort().join('::');const edge=s.links[k]||(s.links[k]={a,b,sharedTurns:0,provenance:'Observed nearby discussion'});edge.sharedTurns=Math.min(1000,edge.sharedTurns+1);ledger(chat,'conversation',`Nearby discussion: ${chat.npc.name} and ${participant.name||b}`,'Observed two participants');persist(chat);}
  const result=(ok,message,detail)=>({ok,message,detail});
  function activityInfo(chat){const c=context(chat.npc),s=state(chat),room=norm(c.place);const task=c.inside?BUILDING_ACTIVITIES.find(x=>x.parent===c.region?.id&&x.place.test(room)):null;const reg=task||c.region;return {c,reg,progress:reg?s.activity[reg.id]:null};}
  function startActivity(chat){const {c,reg,progress}=activityInfo(chat);if(!reg)return result(false,'Current map is not identified.');
    // English Town has a room-specific postal activity. Other regions use a region-specific lesson.
    if(reg.place&&(!c.inside||!reg.place.test(norm(c.place))))return result(false,`Go inside the ${reg.venue} to start this activity; your current scene is ${c.place}.`);
    if(progress?.done)return result(false,'This activity has already been completed; review it in your timeline.');
    const s=state(chat);s.activity[reg.id]={step:0,startedDay:c.day,done:false};ledger(chat,'activity-start',`Started ${reg.title}`,'Player selected Start');return result(true,`Started: ${reg.title}`);
  }
  function tryActivity(chat,sentence){const {c,reg,progress}=activityInfo(chat);if(!reg||!progress||progress.done)return result(false,'Start an available activity first.');
    if(reg.place&&(!c.inside||!reg.place.test(norm(c.place))))return result(false,'Return to the required building to continue.');
    const phrase=String(sentence||'').trim();if(phrase.length<8||phrase.length>280)return result(false,'Write a short English sentence (8–280 characters).');
    const step=reg.steps[progress.step];if(!step)return result(false,'Step unavailable.');
    if(!step[2].test(phrase))return result(false,`Not yet. Focus on: ${step[0]}. You can use the example as a guide.`);
    progress.step++;ledger(chat,'practice',`${reg.title}: ${step[0]}`,'User submitted an English sentence / local pattern check');
    if(progress.step>=reg.steps.length){progress.done=true;progress.finishedDay=c.day;state(chat).world[reg.id]={completed:true,effect:reg.effect,day:c.day};ledger(chat,'world-change',reg.effect,'V80 activity completed · visible state');return result(true,`Completed: ${reg.effect}. This is a V80 side activity; original quest rewards remain unchanged.`,true);}
    persist(chat);return result(true,`Step ${progress.step}/${reg.steps.length} confirmed. Try the next instruction.`);
  }
  function caseState(chat,which){const s=state(chat);return s.investigations[which.id]||(s.investigations[which.id]={clues:[],solved:false});}
  function addClue(chat,which,index,classification){const c=context(chat.npc);if(which.area!==c.region?.id)return result(false,'This case belongs to another region.');const q=caseState(chat,which);if(q.solved)return result(false,'Case already recorded as solved.');if(!Number.isInteger(index)||index<0||index>=which.clues.length)return result(false,'Invalid clue.');if(q.clues.includes(index))return result(false,'This clue is already in your notebook.');if(classification!==which.clues[index][2])return result(false,'Check whether this is an observation, an inference, or an unverified claim.');q.clues.push(index);ledger(chat,'evidence',`${which.title}: ${which.clues[index][0]}`,'V80 authored practice clue');return result(true,'Practice clue recorded. It is part of a fictional in-game exercise.');}
  function solveCase(chat,which,answer){const c=context(chat.npc);if(which.area!==c.region?.id)return result(false,'Travel to the case region first.');const q=caseState(chat,which);if(q.solved)return result(false,'Already solved.');if(q.clues.length<3)return result(false,'Collect and compare all three clues first.');if(!which.solution.test(answer||''))return result(false,'Try expressing the need to check or compare the available evidence.');q.solved=true;ledger(chat,'investigation',`Practice case completed: ${which.title}`,'Clue-based V80 exercise');return result(true,'Excellent: you checked the evidence without inventing facts.');}
  function observeEvent(chat){const c=context(chat.npc),r=c.region;if(!r)return result(false,'Region not confirmed.');const phase=c.minutes<660?'preparation':c.minutes<1080?'community hours':'closing';return {name:`${r.label} community activity`,phase,label:'V80 scheduled practice (not a live world event)',people:c.nearby.length,activity:c.activity||'No NPC activity confirmed'};}
  function namedButtons(parent,text,fn){const b=el('button','lq80-button',text);b.type='button';b.addEventListener('click',fn);parent.append(b);return b;}
  const line=(parent,title,value,kind='')=>{const d=el('div','lq80-line'+(kind?' '+kind:''));d.append(el('strong','',title),el('span','',value));parent.append(d);return d;};
  const action=(parent,title,description,fn)=>{const d=el('div','lq80-action');d.append(el('strong','',title),el('p','',description));namedButtons(d,'Open',fn);parent.append(d);};
  const UI=new WeakMap();
  const sections=[['world','World'],['activities','Activities'],['choices','Choices'],['cases','Evidence'],['people','People'],['coach','English'],['timeline','Timeline']];
  function render(chat){const u=UI.get(chat);if(!u||!chat.npc)return;const {panel,buttons,view}=u,c=context(chat.npc),s=state(chat);view.replaceChildren();for(const b of buttons)b.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(b.dataset.section===u.tab));
    if(u.tab==='world'){
      line(view,'Verified position',c.place,'strong');line(view,'Current region',c.region?.label||'Unidentified');line(view,'NPC observed activity',c.activity||'Unavailable · no claim');line(view,'Nearby visible NPCs',c.nearby.map(x=>x.name).join(', ')||'None confirmed');
      const event=observeEvent(chat);if(event.ok===false)line(view,'World events','No verified event');else{line(view,'Community schedule',`${event.phase} · ${event.label}`);line(view,'Simulation','Event times describe V80 practice, not a real-time festival.');}
      const effects=Object.values(s.world).filter(x=>x?.completed&&x.regionId===c.region?.id);line(view,'Visible changes',effects.length?effects.map(x=>x.effect).join(' · '):'Complete an activity to record a local change.');
      line(view,'Background source','Reconstructed 2.5D using the actual NPC position, facing, room objects, game time and weather. Some facade details are artistic reconstructions.');
      line(view,'NPC reaction style',styleSignature(chat.npc)+' · individuality, not assumed from profession');
      const controls=el('div','lq80-row');namedButtons(controls,'Reduced motion: '+(s.settings.motion?'OFF':'ON'),()=>{s.settings.motion=!s.settings.motion;persist(chat);global.LQ78Motion?.toggle?.(s.settings.motion);render(chat);});for(const v of ['low','balanced','high']){const b=namedButtons(controls,v[0].toUpperCase()+v.slice(1),()=>{s.settings.quality=v;global.LQ79?.setQuality?.(v);persist(chat);render(chat);});b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(s.settings.quality===v));}view.append(controls);
    }
    if(u.tab==='activities'){
      const r=c.region;if(!r){line(view,'Activity unavailable','The game has not identified your current region.');return;}
      const status=s.activity[r.id]||null,idx=status?.step||0;
      line(view,r.icon+' '+r.title,r.purpose,'strong');line(view,'Learning focus',r.grammar);line(view,'Where',r.place?r.venue+' (must be inside)':r.label+' region');line(view,'Progress',status?.done?'Completed':status?`${idx} / ${r.steps.length} steps`:'Not started');
      if(status?.done){line(view,'World state',s.world[r.id]?.effect||'Recorded');return;}
      if(!status){namedButtons(view,'Start activity',()=>{notify(chat,startActivity(chat));render(chat);});return;}
      const step=r.steps[Math.min(idx,r.steps.length-1)];line(view,'Step '+(idx+1),step[0]);line(view,'Example',step[1]);const input=el('textarea','lq80-answer');input.rows=2;input.maxLength=280;input.placeholder='Write your own sentence in English';input.setAttribute('aria-label','Activity answer');view.append(input);
      const controls=el('div','lq80-row');namedButtons(controls,'Use example',()=>{input.value=step[1];input.focus();});namedButtons(controls,'Check sentence',()=>{notify(chat,tryActivity(chat,input.value));render(chat);});view.append(controls);
      line(view,'Assessment','Local phrase patterns, not a full semantic or CEFR assessment. No original quest rewards are granted.');
    }
    if(u.tab==='choices'){
      line(view,'Speak in your own way','Choose a style, then edit the sentence before sending. NPC replies follow the original conversation engine.','strong');
      const r=c.region,role=safe(chat.npc?.name||'Resident',50);
      const options=[
        ['Polite','Could you please explain what is happening here?','A considerate request'],
        ['Curious','I noticed you seem busy. What are you working on?','A question grounded in what you can observe'],
        ['Thoughtful','I understand your point, but could we consider another idea?','A respectful disagreement'],
        ['Supportive','Would you like some help with your work?','An offer, not a confirmed action']
      ];
      if(r?.id==='tech-co')options.push(['Professional','Could we meet and discuss the problem?','Workplace B1']);
      else if(r?.id==='wish-valley')options.push(['Advice','If I were you, I would listen to the community.','Wish Valley B2']);
      else if(r?.id==='vocalt')options.push(['Negotiation','Could we confirm the price before we agree?','Greedy Land']);
      for(const [label,sentence,desc] of options){const d=el('div','lq80-action');d.append(el('strong','',label),el('p','',desc));namedButtons(d,'Edit this response',()=>{chat.input.value=sentence;u.panel.hidden=true;u.toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');chat.input.focus();});view.append(d);}
      line(view,'Outcome','Your wording is saved as an observed turn, not a promise or an automatic mission result.');
    }
    if(u.tab==='cases'){
      const avail=CASES.filter(q=>q.area===c.region?.id);
      if(!avail.length){line(view,'Evidence workshop','Cases are currently offered in Old Oriaris, Wish Valley and Vocalt.');return;}
      for(const q of avail){const data=caseState(chat,q);line(view,q.title,q.question,'strong');line(view,'Status',data.solved?'Completed':data.clues.length+' / 3 clues');line(view,'Scope','A fictional educational side case, not historical evidence.');for(let i=0;i<q.clues.length;i++){
        const [name,clue]=q.clues[i];const div=el('div','lq80-clue');div.append(el('strong','',name),el('p','',clue));
        if(!data.clues.includes(i)&&!data.solved){
          const select=el('select','lq80-classify');select.setAttribute('aria-label','Classify '+name);
          for(const [id,label] of [['','Choose evidence type'],['observation','Observed in the case'],['inference','Inferred / possible'],['unverified','Unverified claim']]){const o=el('option','',label);o.value=id;select.append(o);}div.append(select);
          namedButtons(div,'Classify & record',()=>{notify(chat,addClue(chat,q,i,select.value));if(caseState(chat,q).clues.includes(i))render(chat);});
        }else div.append(el('small','',`✓ Classified: ${q.clues[i][2]}`));view.append(div);
      }if(data.solved)continue;
      const input=el('textarea','lq80-answer');input.rows=2;input.placeholder='How would you verify the evidence?';input.setAttribute('aria-label','Case reasoning');view.append(input);namedButtons(view,'Check reasoning',()=>{notify(chat,solveCase(chat,q,input.value));render(chat);});}
    }
    if(u.tab==='people'){
      line(view,'Conversation partners',`${Object.keys(s.contacts).length} NPCs met · only in this save`,'strong');
      for(const x of Object.values(s.contacts).slice(-14)){line(view,x.met||'Resident',`${x.turns||0} observed turns · last: day ${x.lastDay||x.firstDay||'?'} · response tone ${x.lastTone||'undetermined'}`);}
      line(view,'Observed group conversations',Object.keys(s.links).length+' pair(s)');
      for(const x of Object.values(s.links).slice(-8))line(view,`${s.contacts[x.a]?.met||x.a} ↔ ${s.contacts[x.b]?.met||x.b}`,`${x.sharedTurns} joint practice turns · not proof of friendship`);
      line(view,'Nearby residents',c.nearby.map(x=>x.name).join(', ')||'None currently observed');
      let lifeStage='Not specified';try{const a=typeof lq78Age==='function'?lq78Age(chat.npc.id):null;if(a)lifeStage=String(a)+' (portrait design, not biography)';}catch{}
      line(view,'Current NPC life-stage design',lifeStage);
      if(/elder|mature|adult/i.test(lifeStage))namedButtons(view,'Ask about earlier times',()=>{chat.input.value='What has changed here since you were younger?';u.panel.hidden=true;u.toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');chat.input.focus();});
      line(view,'Different generations','Stories and memories must come from the authored game lore or confirmed NPC dialogue; appearance alone is not historical evidence.');
    }
    if(u.tab==='coach'){
      line(view,'Personal English coach','Feedback counts come from saved correction cards; not a CEFR placement test.','strong');
      const rank=Object.entries(s.coach).sort((a,b)=>b[1].count-a[1].count).slice(0,5);
      if(!rank.length)line(view,'No corrected sentences','Turn Teacher ON and practise; corrections will appear here.');
      for(const [skill,x] of rank){line(view,skill.replaceAll('-',' '),`${x.count} feedback item(s) · ${x.recent.at(-1)||''}`);}
      const rec=rank[0]?.[0]||'sentence-clarity',phrases={'past-continuous':'While I was walking, my friend called me.','conditionals':'If I were you, I would explain the reason.','polite-requests':'Could you please tell me more?','sentence-clarity':'I would like to explain my idea clearly.'};
      line(view,'Suggested practice',phrases[rec]);namedButtons(view,'Try this sentence',()=>{chat.input.value=phrases[rec];u.panel.hidden=true;u.toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');chat.input.focus();});
      line(view,'CEFR policy','Only the existing game progression system assesses level. V80 does not award or inflate CEFR.');
    }
    if(u.tab==='timeline'){
      line(view,'Evidence timeline','Fact / statement / practice completion are labelled separately.','strong');
      if(!s.ledger.length)line(view,'No records','Start an interaction or activity to build a timeline.');
      for(const x of s.ledger.slice(-24).reverse())line(view,`Day ${x.day} · ${String(Math.floor(x.minutes/60)).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(x.minutes%60).padStart(2,'0')} · ${x.kind}`,`${x.label} — ${x.source}`);
      const bottom=el('div','lq80-row');namedButtons(bottom,'Export timeline',()=>{const data=JSON.stringify({version:80,events:s.ledger},null,2);const blob=new Blob([data],{type:'application/json'});const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);const a=el('a');a.href=url;a.download='lifequest-v80-timeline.json';document.body.append(a);a.click();a.remove();setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000);});namedButtons(bottom,'Clear V80 timeline',()=>{if(!confirm('Clear V80 timeline records? This will not erase the base game save.'))return;s.ledger=[];persist(chat);render(chat);});view.append(bottom);
    }
  }
  function notify(chat,res){const u=UI.get(chat);if(!u||!res)return;u.feedback.textContent=res.message||'';u.feedback.dataset.ok=String(!!res.ok);u.feedback.setAttribute('role','status');}
  let mapMark=null,markedScene=null;
  function updateMapMarker(sc,r,w){
    try{
      if((markedScene!==sc||!r||!w?.completed) && mapMark){mapMark.destroy?.();mapMark=null;markedScene=null;}
      if(!sc||!r||!w?.completed||!w.actorId||!sc.add?.text)return;
      const live=(sc.npcs||[]).find(n=>n.def?.id===w.actorId&&n.sprite?.visible&&!!n.inside===!!sc.inside);
      if(!live||!sc.player||Math.hypot(live.sprite.x-sc.player.x,live.sprite.y-sc.player.y)>210){if(mapMark){mapMark.destroy?.();mapMark=null;}return;}
      if(!mapMark){mapMark=sc.add.text(live.sprite.x,live.sprite.y-30,'✓',{fontFamily:'monospace',fontSize:'12px',fontStyle:'bold',color:'#b1f3cc',stroke:'#162a30',strokeThickness:3});mapMark.setOrigin?.(.5,1);mapMark.setDepth?.(999);markedScene=sc;}
      mapMark.setPosition?.(live.sprite.x,live.sprite.y-30);
    }catch{if(mapMark){try{mapMark.destroy?.();}catch{}mapMark=null;}}
  }
  function updateBadge(chat){
    let b=document.getElementById('lq80-world-badge');if(!b){b=el('div','lq80-world-badge');b.id='lq80-world-badge';b.setAttribute('aria-live','off');b.hidden=true;document.body.append(b);}
    const sc=scene(),r=regionOf(sc),w=Object.values(state(chat).world).filter(x=>x?.completed&&x.regionId===r?.id).at(-1);updateMapMarker(sc,r,w);
    const chatVisible=!!chat.npc&&chat.root?.getClientRects?.().length>0;
    if(!sc||!r||!w?.completed||chatVisible){b.hidden=true;return;}
    b.textContent='✓ '+r.label+' · '+w.effect;
    b.hidden=false;
  }
  function setup(chat){const root=chat.root;if(!root||UI.has(chat))return;const top=$(root,'.lq772-titlebar');if(!top)return;const toggle=el('button','lq80-toggle','World & learning');toggle.type='button';toggle.id='lq80-toggle';toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');const panel=el('aside','lq80-panel');panel.id='lq80-panel';panel.hidden=true;panel.setAttribute('aria-label','LifeQuest world and learning');toggle.setAttribute('aria-controls','lq80-panel');
    const title=el('div','lq80-panel-title');title.append(el('strong','',VERSION));const close=namedButtons(title,'✕ Close',()=>{panel.hidden=true;toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');chat.input.focus();});close.setAttribute('aria-label','Close World and learning');panel.append(title);
    const tabs=el('div','lq80-tabs');tabs.setAttribute('role','tablist');const buttons=[];const view=el('div','lq80-content');view.setAttribute('role','tabpanel');const feedback=el('div','lq80-feedback');feedback.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');

            setupMic() {
              let e = window,
                t = e.SpeechRecognition ?? e.webkitSpeechRecognition;
              if (!t) {

                }),

                        )

                          )
